    1: import { useEffect } from 'react'
    2: import { ConfigProvider } from 'antd'
    3: import viVN from 'antd/locale/vi_VN'
    4: import enUS from 'antd/locale/en_US'
    5: import { useAuth } from '@/hooks/useAuth'
    6: import { useLocaleStore } from '@/stores/localeStore'
    7: import AppRouter from '@/routes'
    8: import ErrorBoundary from '@/components/feedback/ErrorBoundary'
    9: 
   10: function App() {
   11:   const { fetchMe, initialized } = useAuth()
   12:   const { locale } = useLocaleStore()
   13:   useEffect(() => { if (!initialized) fetchMe() }, [initialized, fetchMe])
   14: 
   15:   return (
   16:     <ErrorBoundary>
   17:       <ConfigProvider locale={locale === 'en' ? enUS : viVN} theme={{ token: { colorPrimary: '#1677ff', borderRadius: 6 } }}>
   18:         <AppRouter />
   19:       </ConfigProvider>
   20:     </ErrorBoundary>
   21:   )
   22: }
   23: export default App

    1: import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
    2: import { FaRobot, FaPaperPlane, FaSpinner, FaTimes, FaTrash } from 'react-icons/fa';
    3: import { useAuth } from '@/hooks/useAuth';
    4: import { useAIAgent } from '@/hooks/useAIAgent';
    5: 
    6: const AIAgentWidget = () => {
    7:   const { isAuthenticated } = useAuth();
    8:   const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
    9:   const [input, setInput] = useState('');
   10:   const { messages, loading, send, clear } = useAIAgent();
   11:   const listRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
   12: 
   13:   useEffect(() => {
   14:     listRef.current?.scrollTo({
   15:       top: listRef.current.scrollHeight,
   16:       behavior: 'smooth',
   17:     });
   18:   }, [messages]);
   19: 
   20:   const submit = () => {
   21:     if (!input.trim() || loading) return;
   22:     void send(input);
   23:     setInput('');
   24:   };
   25: 
   26:   if (!isAuthenticated) return null;
   27: 
   28:   return (
   29:     <>
   30:       <button
   31:         type="button"
   32:         onClick={() => setOpen((v) => !v)}
   33:         className="relative inline-flex h-10 w-10 items-center justify-center rounded-[10px] border-0 bg-transparent text-slate-600 transition-colors hover:bg-slate-100 hover:text-brand-700"
   34:         title="Trợ lý AI"
   35:         aria-label="Trợ lý AI"
   36:       >
   37:         <FaRobot size={18} />
   38:       </button>
   39: 
   40:       {open && (
   41:         <div className="fixed bottom-5 right-5 z-[1200] flex h-[600px] w-[400px] max-h-[calc(100vh-24px)] max-w-[calc(100vw-24px)] flex-col overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white shadow-[0_24px_48px_-18px_rgba(15,23,42,0.30),0_2px_10px_rgba(15,23,42,0.06)]">
   42:           {/* Header */}
   43:           <div className="flex h-14 shrink-0 items-center justify-between bg-brand px-3 text-white">
   44:             <div className="flex min-w-0 items-center gap-2.5">
   45:               <div className="flex h-8 w-8 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-white/25">
   46:                 <FaRobot size={15} />
   47:               </div>
   48:               <div className="min-w-0">
   49:                 <div className="truncate text-[13.5px] font-bold leading-[18px]">
   50:                   Trợ lý ECMS
   51:                 </div>
   52:                 <div className="truncate text-[11px] leading-[15px] text-white/80">
   53:                   Hỏi tôi về Lead, khóa học, học viên…
   54:                 </div>
   55:               </div>
   56:             </div>
   57:             <div className="flex shrink-0 items-center gap-0.5">
   58:               {messages.length > 0 && (
   59:                 <button
   60:                   type="button"
   61:                   onClick={clear}
   62:                   className="inline-flex h-[30px] w-[30px] items-center justify-center rounded-[9px] bg-transparent text-white transition-colors hover:bg-white/20"
   63:                   title="Xoá hội thoại"
   64:                 >
   65:                   <FaTrash size={12} />
   66:                 </button>
   67:               )}
   68:               <button
   69:                 type="button"
   70:                 onClick={() => setOpen(false)}
   71:                 className="inline-flex h-[30px] w-[30px] items-center justify-center rounded-[9px] bg-transparent text-white transition-colors hover:bg-white/20"
   72:                 title="Đóng"
   73:               >
   74:                 <FaTimes size={14} />
   75:               </button>
   76:             </div>
   77:           </div>
   78: 
   79:           {/* Messages */}
   80:           <div
   81:             ref={listRef}
   82:             className="flex min-h-0 flex-1 flex-col gap-2 overflow-y-auto bg-slate-50 p-3"
   83:           >
   84:             {messages.length === 0 && (
   85:               <div className="flex h-full flex-col items-center justify-center gap-1 text-center">
   86:                 <FaRobot size={28} className="mb-1 text-slate-300" />
   87:                 <div className="text-[12.5px] font-semibold text-slate-500">
   88:                   Xin chào 👋
   89:                 </div>
   90:                 <div className="max-w-[260px] text-[11px] text-slate-400">
   91:                   Thử hỏi: "Tạo lead Nguyễn Văn A, SĐT 0901234567"
   92:                 </div>
   93:               </div>
   94:             )}
   95: 
   96:             {messages.map((m) => {
   97:               if (m.pending) {
   98:                 return (
   99:                   <div key={m.id} className="flex justify-start">
  100:                     <div className="rounded-2xl rounded-bl-sm border border-slate-200 bg-white px-3 py-2 text-[12px] text-slate-400">
  101:                       <FaSpinner className="mr-1 inline animate-spin" size={10} />
  102:                       Đang suy nghĩ...
  103:                     </div>
  104:                   </div>
  105:                 );
  106:               }
  107:               if (m.error) {
  108:                 return (
  109:                   <div key={m.id} className="flex justify-start">
  110:                     <div className="rounded-2xl rounded-bl-sm border border-rose-200 bg-rose-50 px-3 py-2 text-[12px] text-rose-600">
  111:                       ⚠️ {m.error}
  112:                     </div>
  113:                   </div>
  114:                 );
  115:               }
  116:               const isUser = m.role === 'user';
  117:               return (
  118:                 <div
  119:                   key={m.id}
  120:                   className={`flex ${isUser ? 'justify-end' : 'justify-start'}`}
  121:                 >
  122:                   <div
  123:                     className={`max-w-[80%] whitespace-pre-wrap break-words rounded-2xl px-3 py-2 text-[13px] leading-[1.45] ${
  124:                       isUser
  125:                         ? 'rounded-br-[5px] bg-brand text-white'
  126:                         : 'rounded-bl-[5px] border border-slate-200/80 bg-white text-slate-700'
  127:                     }`}
  128:                   >
  129:                     {m.content}
  130:                   </div>
  131:                 </div>
  132:               );
  133:             })}
  134:           </div>
  135: 
  136:           {/* Input */}
  137:           <form
  138:             onSubmit={(e) => {
  139:               e.preventDefault();
  140:               submit();
  141:             }}
  142:             className="flex shrink-0 items-end gap-2 border-t border-slate-200 bg-white px-3 pb-2.5 pt-2"
  143:           >
  144:             <textarea
  145:               rows={1}
  146:               value={input}
  147:               onChange={(e) => setInput(e.target.value)}
  148:               onKeyDown={(e) => {
  149:                 if (e.key === 'Enter' && !e.shiftKey) {
  150:                   e.preventDefault();
  151:                   submit();
  152:                 }
  153:               }}
  154:               disabled={loading}
  155:               maxLength={2000}
  156:               placeholder="Nhập tin nhắn... (Shift + Enter để xuống dòng)"
  157:               className="min-h-9 max-h-24 flex-1 resize-none rounded-xl border border-slate-200 px-2.5 py-2 text-[13px] leading-[18px] text-slate-800 outline-none transition placeholder:text-slate-400 focus:border-brand focus:ring-[3px] focus:ring-brand/15 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:bg-slate-50"
  158:             />
  159:             <button
  160:               type="submit"
  161:               disabled={loading || !input.trim()}
  162:               className="inline-flex h-9 w-9 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-brand text-white transition-colors hover:bg-brand-600 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-45"
  163:               title="Gửi"
  164:             >
  165:               {loading ? (
  166:                 <FaSpinner className="animate-spin" size={12} />
  167:               ) : (
  168:                 <FaPaperPlane size={12} />
  169:               )}
  170:             </button>
  171:           </form>
  172:         </div>
  173:       )}
  174:     </>
  175:   );
  176: };
  177: 
  178: export default AIAgentWidget;

// ============================================================
// FILE: ./src/components/chat/AddMembersView.tsx
// ============================================================
    1: // src/components/chat/AddMembersView.tsx
    2: import { useState } from 'react';
    3: import { FaSpinner, FaTimes, FaUserPlus } from 'react-icons/fa';
    4: import type { ParticipantResponse, UserSearchResponse } from '@/services/types/chat';
    5: import UserPicker from './UserPicker';
    6: 
    7: interface Props {
    8:   /** Nhóm hiện tại — dùng để loại các thành viên đã có */
    9:   participants: ParticipantResponse[];
   10:   /** useChat().handleAddParticipants */
   11:   onAdd: (accountIds: number[]) => Promise<void>;
   12:   /** Quay lại danh sách thành viên */
   13:   onDone: () => void;
   14: }
   15: 
   16: /**
   17:  * View thêm thành viên vào nhóm.
   18:  * BE: POST /api/chat/conversations/{id}/participants { accountIds }
   19:  * (chỉ OWNER/ADMIN, không áp dụng cho PRIVATE)
   20:  */
   21: const AddMembersView = ({ participants, onAdd, onDone }: Props) => {
   22:   const [selected, setSelected] = useState<UserSearchResponse[]>([]);
   23:   const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);
   24:   const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
   25: 
   26:   const existingIds = participants.map((p) => p.accountId);
   27:   const selectedIds = selected.map((u) => u.accountId);
   28: 
   29:   const toggle = (u: UserSearchResponse) => {
   30:     setError(null);
   31:     setSelected((prev) =>
   32:       prev.some((x) => x.accountId === u.accountId)
   33:         ? prev.filter((x) => x.accountId !== u.accountId)
   34:         : [...prev, u],
   35:     );
   36:   };
   37: 
   38:   const handleSubmit = async () => {
   39:     if (selectedIds.length === 0) {
   40:       setError('Vui lòng chọn ít nhất 1 người');
   41:       return;
   42:     }
   43:     setSubmitting(true);
   44:     setError(null);
   45:     try {
   46:       await onAdd(selectedIds);
   47:       setSelected([]);
   48:       onDone();
   49:     } catch (err) {
   50:       setError((err as { message?: string })?.message || 'Không thể thêm thành viên');
   51:     } finally {
   52:       setSubmitting(false);
   53:     }
   54:   };
   55: 
   56:   return (
   57:     <div className="flex min-h-0 flex-1 flex-col bg-white">
   58:       {selected.length > 0 && (
   59:         <div className="shrink-0 border-b border-slate-100 px-3 pb-2.5 pt-2.5">
   60:           <div className="flex max-h-[76px] flex-wrap gap-1 overflow-y-auto">
   61:             <span className="self-center text-[10.5px] font-semibold text-slate-500">
   62:               Đã chọn {selected.length}:
   63:             </span>
   64:             {selected.map((u) => (
   65:               <span
   66:                 key={u.accountId}
   67:                 className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded-full bg-brand/10 py-0.5 pl-2 pr-1 text-[10.5px] font-semibold leading-4 text-brand-700"
   68:               >
   69:                 <span className="max-w-[120px] truncate">
   70:                   {u.fullName || u.username || `User #${u.accountId}`}
   71:                 </span>
   72:                 <button
   73:                   type="button"
   74:                   onClick={() =>
   75:                     setSelected((prev) => prev.filter((x) => x.accountId !== u.accountId))
   76:                   }
   77:                   className="rounded-full border-0 bg-transparent p-0.5 transition-colors hover:bg-brand/20"
   78:                   title="Bỏ chọn"
   79:                 >
   80:                   <FaTimes size={8} />
   81:                 </button>
   82:               </span>
   83:             ))}
   84:           </div>
   85:         </div>
   86:       )}
   87: 
   88:       <UserPicker
   89:         selectedIds={selectedIds}
   90:         onToggle={toggle}
   91:         excludeIds={existingIds}
   92:         autoFocus
   93:         placeholder="Tìm người để thêm vào nhóm..."
   94:         emptyHint="Không còn người phù hợp để thêm"
   95:         idleHint="Nhập ít nhất 2 ký tự để tìm người dùng"
   96:       />
   97: 
   98:       <div className="shrink-0 border-t border-slate-200 px-3 pb-2.5 pt-2">
   99:         {error && <div className="mb-1.5 text-[11px] font-semibold text-rose-500">{error}</div>}
  100:         <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
  101:           <button
  102:             type="button"
  103:             onClick={handleSubmit}
  104:             disabled={submitting || selectedIds.length === 0}
  105:             className="inline-flex h-9 flex-1 items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl bg-brand px-3 text-xs font-bold text-white transition-colors hover:bg-brand-600 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-45"
  106:           >
  107:             {submitting ? (
  108:               <FaSpinner className="animate-spin" size={12} />
  109:             ) : (
  110:               <FaUserPlus size={12} />
  111:             )}
  112:             Thêm vào nhóm
  113:           </button>
  114:           <span className="shrink-0 whitespace-nowrap text-[10.5px] text-slate-400">
  115:             Nhóm có {participants.length} người
  116:           </span>
  117:         </div>
  118:       </div>
  119:     </div>
  120:   );
  121: };
  122: 
  123: export default AddMembersView;

// FILE: ./src/components/chat/chatHelpers.ts
// ============================================================
    1: // src/components/chat/chatHelpers.ts
    2: import type { ConversationResponse } from '@/services/types/chat';
    3: 
    4: /** "vừa xong" / "5p" / "3g" / "12/03" */
    5: export const formatRelative = (iso?: string | null) => {
    6:   if (!iso) return '';
    7:   const time = new Date(iso).getTime();
    8:   if (Number.isNaN(time)) return '';
    9:   const minutes = Math.floor((Date.now() - time) / 60000);
   10:   if (minutes < 1) return 'vừa xong';
   11:   if (minutes < 60) return `${minutes}p`;
   12:   const hours = Math.floor(minutes / 60);
   13:   if (hours < 24) return `${hours}g`;
   14:   const days = Math.floor(hours / 24);
   15:   if (days < 7) return `${days}d`;
   16:   return new Date(iso).toLocaleDateString('vi-VN', { day: '2-digit', month: '2-digit' });
   17: };
   18: 
   19: export const formatClock = (iso?: string | null) => {
   20:   if (!iso) return '';
   21:   const d = new Date(iso);
   22:   if (Number.isNaN(d.getTime())) return '';
   23:   return d.toLocaleTimeString('vi-VN', { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' });
   24: };
   25: 
   26: const startOfDay = (d: Date) =>
   27:   new Date(d.getFullYear(), d.getMonth(), d.getDate()).getTime();
   28: 
   29: /** Khoá theo ngày (dùng để chèn dải ngày) */
   30: export const dayKey = (iso?: string | null) => {
   31:   if (!iso) return '';
   32:   const d = new Date(iso);
   33:   if (Number.isNaN(d.getTime())) return '';
   34:   return `${d.getFullYear()}-${d.getMonth() + 1}-${d.getDate()}`;
   35: };
   36: 
   37: /** "Hôm nay" / "Hôm qua" / "12/03/2026" */
   38: export const formatDayLabel = (iso?: string | null) => {
   39:   if (!iso) return '';
   40:   const d = new Date(iso);
   41:   if (Number.isNaN(d.getTime())) return '';
   42:   const diffDays = Math.round((startOfDay(new Date()) - startOfDay(d)) / 86400000);
   43:   if (diffDays === 0) return 'Hôm nay';
   44:   if (diffDays === 1) return 'Hôm qua';
   45:   return d.toLocaleDateString('vi-VN', {
   46:     day: '2-digit',
   47:     month: '2-digit',
   48:     year: 'numeric',
   49:   });
   50: };
   51: 
   52: export const getConvIcon = (type: string) => {
   53:   if (type === 'COURSE') return '📚';
   54:   if (type === 'GROUP') return '👥';
   55:   return '👤';
   56: };
   57: 
   58: /** BE đã tự tính name cho PRIVATE (tên người còn lại) */
   59: export const getConversationTitle = (
   60:   conversation: ConversationResponse,
   61:   currentUserId?: number,
   62: ) => {
   63:   if (conversation.name) return conversation.name;
   64:   if (conversation.conversationType === 'PRIVATE') {
   65:     const other = (conversation.participants || []).find(
   66:       (p) => p.accountId !== currentUserId,
   67:     );
   68:     if (other) return other.fullName || other.username || 'Trò chuyện';
   69:   }
   70:   return `Hội thoại #${conversation.id}`;
   71: };
   72: 
   73: /** Avatar của người còn lại trong chat riêng (nhóm dùng emoji) */
   74: export const getConversationAvatar = (
   75:   conversation: ConversationResponse,
   76:   currentUserId?: number,
   77: ) => {
   78:   if (conversation.conversationType !== 'PRIVATE') return null;
   79:   const other = (conversation.participants || []).find((p) => p.accountId !== currentUserId);
   80:   return other?.avatarUrl || null;
   81: };
   82: 
   83: /**
   84:  * Toạ độ cho dropdown dạng `position: fixed` — tránh bị cắt bởi vùng
   85:  * `overflow-y-auto` (danh sách hội thoại / thành viên) và luôn nằm trong viewport.
   86:  */
   87: export const anchorMenu = (el: HTMLElement, width: number, height = 260) => {
   88:   const rect = el.getBoundingClientRect();
   89:   const top = Math.max(8, Math.min(rect.bottom + 6, window.innerHeight - height - 8));
   90:   const left = Math.max(8, Math.min(rect.right - width, window.innerWidth - width - 8));
   91:   return { top, left };
   92: };

// ============================================================
// FILE: ./src/components/chat/ChatMessageInput.tsx
// ============================================================
    1: // src/components/chat/ChatMessageInput.tsx
    2: import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
    3: import type { KeyboardEvent } from 'react';
    4: import { FaLock, FaPaperPlane, FaReply, FaSpinner, FaTimes } from 'react-icons/fa';
    5: import type { MessageResponse } from '@/services/types/chat';
    6: 
    7: interface Props {
    8:   onSend: (content: string) => void | Promise<void>;
    9:   onTyping?: () => void;
   10:   disabled?: boolean;
   11:   disabledReason?: string;
   12:   replyingTo?: MessageResponse | null;
   13:   onCancelReply?: () => void;
   14: }
   15: 
   16: const MAX_LENGTH = 10000;
   17: 
   18: const ChatMessageInput = ({
   19:   onSend,
   20:   onTyping,
   21:   disabled = false,
   22:   disabledReason,
   23:   replyingTo,
   24:   onCancelReply,
   25: }: Props) => {
   26:   const [text, setText] = useState('');
   27:   const [sending, setSending] = useState(false);
   28:   const areaRef = useRef<HTMLTextAreaElement>(null);
   29: 
   30:   // Auto-resize (1 → 4 dòng)
   31:   useEffect(() => {
   32:     const el = areaRef.current;
   33:     if (!el) return;
   34:     el.style.height = 'auto';
   35:     el.style.height = `${Math.min(el.scrollHeight, 96)}px`;
   36:   }, [text]);
   37: 
   38:   const submit = async () => {
   39:     if (disabled || sending) return;
   40:     const trimmed = text.trim();
   41:     if (!trimmed) return;
   42: 
   43:     setSending(true);
   44:     try {
   45:       await onSend(trimmed);
   46:       setText('');
   47:       onCancelReply?.();
   48:     } catch {
   49:       // Lỗi đã được toast ở tầng useChat — giữ nội dung để gửi lại
   50:     } finally {
   51:       setSending(false);
   52:     }
   53:   };
   54: 
   55:   const handleKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent<HTMLTextAreaElement>) => {
   56:     if (e.key !== 'Enter' || e.shiftKey) return;
   57:     // Không gửi khi đang gõ IME (tiếng Việt/Trung/Nhật)
   58:     if (e.nativeEvent.isComposing) return;
   59:     e.preventDefault();
   60:     void submit();
   61:   };
   62: 
   63:   return (
   64:     <form
   65:       onSubmit={(e) => {
   66:         e.preventDefault();
   67:         void submit();
   68:       }}
   69:       className="flex shrink-0 flex-col border-t border-slate-200 bg-white px-3 pb-2.5 pt-2"
   70:     >
   71:       {replyingTo && (
   72:         <div className="mb-1.5 flex items-start gap-1.5 rounded-[7px] border-l-2 border-brand bg-slate-50 px-2 py-1.5">
   73:           <FaReply size={10} className="mt-0.5 shrink-0 text-brand-700" />
   74:           <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
   75:             <div className="text-[10px] font-bold leading-[13px] text-brand-700">
   76:               Trả lời {replyingTo.senderName || 'Ai đó'}
   77:             </div>
   78:             <div className="truncate text-[11px] leading-[15px] text-slate-500">
   79:               {replyingTo.content || `[${replyingTo.messageType}]`}
   80:             </div>
   81:           </div>
   82:           <button
   83:             type="button"
   84:             onClick={onCancelReply}
   85:             className="shrink-0 border-0 bg-transparent p-0.5 text-slate-400 transition-colors hover:text-rose-500"
   86:             title="Huỷ trả lời"
   87:           >
   88:             <FaTimes size={10} />
   89:           </button>
   90:         </div>
   91:       )}
   92: 
   93:       {disabled && disabledReason && (
   94:         <div className="mb-1.5 flex items-start gap-1.5 rounded-[7px] border border-amber-200 bg-amber-50 px-2 py-1.5 text-[11px] leading-[15px] text-amber-700">
   95:           <FaLock size={10} className="mt-0.5 shrink-0" />
   96:           <span>{disabledReason}</span>
   97:         </div>
   98:       )}
   99: 
  100:       {text.length > MAX_LENGTH - 1000 && (
  101:         <div className="mb-1 text-right text-[10px] text-amber-600">
  102:           {text.length}/{MAX_LENGTH}
  103:         </div>
  104:       )}
  105: 
  106:       <div className="flex items-end gap-2">
  107:         <textarea
  108:           ref={areaRef}
  109:           rows={1}
  110:           value={text}
  111:           onChange={(e) => {
  112:             setText(e.target.value);
  113:             onTyping?.();
  114:           }}
  115:           onKeyDown={handleKeyDown}
  116:           disabled={disabled || sending}
  117:           maxLength={MAX_LENGTH}
  118:           placeholder={
  119:             disabled ? 'Không thể gửi tin nhắn' : 'Nhập tin nhắn... (Shift + Enter để xuống dòng)'
  120:           }
  121:           className="min-h-9 max-h-24 flex-1 resize-none rounded-xl border border-slate-200 px-2.5 py-2 text-[13px] leading-[18px] text-slate-800 outline-none transition placeholder:text-slate-400 focus:border-brand focus:ring-[3px] focus:ring-brand/15 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:bg-slate-50 disabled:text-slate-400"
  122:         />
  123:         <button
  124:           type="submit"
  125:           disabled={disabled || sending || !text.trim()}
  126:           className="inline-flex h-9 w-9 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-brand text-white transition-colors hover:bg-brand-600 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-45"
  127:           title="Gửi tin nhắn"
  128:         >
  129:           {sending ? <FaSpinner className="animate-spin" size={12} /> : <FaPaperPlane size={12} />}
  130:         </button>
  131:       </div>
  132:     </form>
  133:   );
  134: };
  135: 
  136: export default ChatMessageInput;

// ============================================================
// FILE: ./src/components/chat/ChatMessageList.tsx
// ============================================================
    1: // src/components/chat/ChatMessageList.tsx
    2: import { useEffect, useMemo, useRef } from 'react';
    3: import { FaArrowUp, FaComments, FaSpinner } from 'react-icons/fa';
    4: import MessageBubble from './MessageBubble';
    5: import { dayKey, formatDayLabel } from './chatHelpers';
    6: import type { MessageResponse, ParticipantResponse } from '@/services/types/chat';
    7: 
    8: interface Props {
    9:   messages: MessageResponse[];
   10:   currentUserId?: number;
   11:   loading?: boolean;
   12:   /** Còn tin nhắn cũ hơn để tải */
   13:   hasMore?: boolean;
   14:   loadingMore?: boolean;
   15:   onLoadMore?: () => Promise<void> | void;
   16:   /** Hiện tên người gửi (chat nhóm) */
   17:   showSender?: boolean;
   18:   /** Cho phép xoá tin của người khác (MODERATOR+) */
   19:   canModerate?: boolean;
   20:   /** Cho phép sửa tin của mình (theo quyền gửi tin của hội thoại) */
   21:   canEdit?: boolean;
   22:   participants?: ParticipantResponse[];
   23:   /** accountId → lastReadMessageId nhận qua WebSocket */
   24:   readReceipts?: Record<number, number | null>;
   25:   onReply?: (message: MessageResponse) => void;
   26:   onEdit?: (id: number, content: string) => void;
   27:   onDelete?: (id: number) => void;
   28: }
   29: 
   30: const ChatMessageList = ({
   31:   messages,
   32:   currentUserId,
   33:   loading = false,
   34:   hasMore = false,
   35:   loadingMore = false,
   36:   onLoadMore,
   37:   showSender = true,
   38:   canModerate = false,
   39:   canEdit = true,
   40:   participants = [],
   41:   readReceipts = {},
   42:   onReply,
   43:   onEdit,
   44:   onDelete,
   45: }: Props) => {
   46:   const listRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
   47:   const endRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
   48: 
   49:   const lastMessageId = messages.length > 0 ? messages[messages.length - 1].id : null;
   50: 
   51:   // Chỉ auto-scroll khi có tin nhắn mới ở cuối (không scroll khi prepend tin cũ)
   52:   useEffect(() => {
   53:     if (lastMessageId == null) return;
   54:     endRef.current?.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'end' });
   55:   }, [lastMessageId]);
   56: 
   57:   // Mốc "đã đọc" của từng thành viên khác
   58:   const readMarks = useMemo(
   59:     () =>
   60:       participants
   61:         .filter((p) => p.accountId !== currentUserId)
   62:         .map((p) => {
   63:           const wsMark = readReceipts[p.accountId];
   64:           const mark = wsMark !== undefined ? wsMark : p.lastReadMessageId;
   65:           return typeof mark === 'number' ? mark : 0;
   66:         })
   67:         .filter((mark) => mark > 0),
   68:     [participants, readReceipts, currentUserId],
   69:   );
   70: 
   71:   const countReaders = (messageId: number) =>
   72:     readMarks.reduce((count, mark) => (mark >= messageId ? count + 1 : count), 0);
   73: 
   74:   const handleLoadMore = async () => {
   75:     if (!onLoadMore || loadingMore) return;
   76:     const el = listRef.current;
   77:     const prevHeight = el?.scrollHeight ?? 0;
   78:     const prevTop = el?.scrollTop ?? 0;
   79:     await onLoadMore();
   80:     // Giữ nguyên vị trí đọc sau khi chèn tin cũ lên đầu
   81:     requestAnimationFrame(() => {
   82:       if (!el) return;
   83:       el.scrollTop = prevTop + (el.scrollHeight - prevHeight);
   84:     });
   85:   };
   86: 
   87:   if (loading && messages.length === 0) {
   88:     return (
   89:       <div className="flex min-h-0 flex-1 items-center justify-center bg-slate-50 text-brand">
   90:         <FaSpinner className="animate-spin" size={22} />
   91:       </div>
   92:     );
   93:   }
   94: 
   95:   if (messages.length === 0) {
   96:     return (
   97:       <div className="flex min-h-0 flex-1 flex-col items-center justify-center gap-1 bg-slate-50 px-6 text-center">
   98:         <FaComments className="mb-1 text-[26px] text-slate-300" />
   99:         <div className="text-[12.5px] font-semibold text-slate-500">Chưa có tin nhắn nào</div>
  100:         <div className="text-[11px] text-slate-400">Hãy gửi tin nhắn đầu tiên</div>
  101:       </div>
  102:     );
  103:   }
  104: 
  105:   return (
  106:     <div
  107:       ref={listRef}
  108:       className="flex min-h-0 flex-1 flex-col gap-2 overflow-y-auto bg-slate-50 p-3"
  109:     >
  110:       {(hasMore || loadingMore) && (
  111:         <button
  112:           type="button"
  113:           onClick={handleLoadMore}
  114:           disabled={loadingMore}
  115:           className="inline-flex shrink-0 items-center gap-1.5 self-center rounded-full border border-slate-200 bg-white px-2.5 py-1 text-[10.5px] font-semibold text-slate-500 transition-colors hover:border-brand hover:text-brand-700 disabled:opacity-65"        >
  116:           {loadingMore ? (
  117:             <FaSpinner className="animate-spin" size={9} />
  118:           ) : (
  119:             <FaArrowUp size={9} />
  120:           )}
  121:           Tải tin nhắn cũ hơn
  122:         </button>
  123:       )}
  124: 
  125:       {messages.map((m, index) => {
  126:         const prev = index > 0 ? messages[index - 1] : null;
  127:         const showDay = dayKey(prev?.createdAt) !== dayKey(m.createdAt);
  128:         const isMine = m.senderId === currentUserId;
  129: 
  130:         return (
  131:           <div key={m.id} className="flex flex-col gap-2">
  132:             {showDay && (
  133:               <div className="self-center rounded-full bg-slate-200/80 px-2.5 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-semibold leading-[15px] text-slate-500">
  134:                 {formatDayLabel(m.createdAt)}
  135:               </div>
  136:             )}
  137:             <MessageBubble
  138:               message={m}
  139:               isMine={isMine}
  140:               showSender={showSender}
  141:               canEdit={isMine && canEdit && !m.isDeleted}
  142:               canDelete={!m.isDeleted && (isMine || canModerate)}
  143:               readBy={isMine ? countReaders(m.id) : 0}
  144:               onReply={onReply}
  145:               onEdit={onEdit}
  146:               onDelete={onDelete}
  147:             />
  148:           </div>
  149:         );
  150:       })}
  151:       <div ref={endRef} />
  152:     </div>
  153:   );
  154: };
  155: 
  156: export default ChatMessageList;

// ============================================================
// FILE: ./src/components/chat/ChatWidget.tsx

    1: // src/components/chat/ChatWidget.tsx
    2: import { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react';
    3: import { FaArchive, FaComments, FaUsers } from 'react-icons/fa';
    4: import toast from 'react-hot-toast';
    5: 
    6: import { useAuth } from '@/hooks/useAuth';
    7: import { useChat } from '@/hooks/useChat';
    8: import { useWebSocket } from '@/hooks/useWebSocket';
    9: import type {
   10:   ConversationResponse,
   11:   MessagePermission,
   12:   MessageResponse,
   13:   ParticipantRole,
   14:   UserSearchResponse,
   15: } from '@/services/types/chat';
   16: 
   17: import ChatWindowHeader from './ChatWindowHeader';
   18: import ConversationList from './ConversationList';
   19: import NewChatView from './NewChatView';
   20: import ChatMessageList from './ChatMessageList';
   21: import ChatMessageInput from './ChatMessageInput';
   22: import MembersView from './MembersView';
   23: import type { MuteDuration } from './MembersView';
   24: import CreateGroupView from './CreateGroupView';
   25: import AddMembersView from './AddMembersView';
   26: import { getConvIcon, getConversationAvatar, getConversationTitle } from './chatHelpers';
   27: 
   28: type ViewMode = 'list' | 'chat' | 'members' | 'addMembers' | 'createGroup';
   29: type ListTab = 'conversations' | 'archived' | 'new';
   30: 
   31: const pad = (n: number) => String(n).padStart(2, '0');
   32: 
   33: /** BE dùng LocalDateTime → gửi chuỗi ISO không có timezone */
   34: const toLocalIso = (d: Date) =>
   35:   `${d.getFullYear()}-${pad(d.getMonth() + 1)}-${pad(d.getDate())}T${pad(d.getHours())}:${pad(
   36:     d.getMinutes(),
   37:   )}:${pad(d.getSeconds())}`;
   38: 
   39: const muteUntilFor = (duration: MuteDuration): string | null => {
   40:   if (duration === 'forever') return null;
   41:   const ms = duration === '1h' ? 60 * 60 * 1000 : 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000;
   42:   return toLocalIso(new Date(Date.now() + ms));
   43: };
   44: 
   45: const MUTE_REASON: Record<MuteDuration, string> = {
   46:   '1h': 'Mute 1 giờ',
   47:   '24h': 'Mute 24 giờ',
   48:   forever: 'Mute vĩnh viễn',
   49: };
   50: 
   51: const ChatWidget = () => {
   52:   const { isAuthenticated } = useAuth();
   53:   const { connected } = useWebSocket();
   54: 
   55:   const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
   56:   const [view, setView] = useState<ViewMode>('list');
   57:   const [tab, setTab] = useState<ListTab>('conversations');
   58:   const [searchTerm, setSearchTerm] = useState('');
   59:   const [replyingTo, setReplyingTo] = useState<MessageResponse | null>(null);
   60:   const [startingId, setStartingId] = useState<number | null>(null);
   61:   const [offline, setOffline] = useState(false);
   62: 
   63:   const notifyError = useCallback((msg: string) => {
   64:     toast.error(msg);
   65:   }, []);
   66: 
   67:   const {
   68:     conversations,
   69:     activeConversation,
   70:     messages,
   71:     participants,
   72:     currentParticipant,
   73:     readReceipts,
   74:     loading,
   75:     loadingMessages,
   76:     loadingMore,
   77:     hasMoreMessages,
   78:     totalUnread,
   79:     currentUserId,
   80:     selectConversation,
   81:     closeConversation,
   82:     fetchConversations,
   83:     loadOlderMessages,
   84:     sendMessage,
   85:     editMessage,
   86:     deleteMessage,
   87:     createGroupConversation,
   88:     startPrivateConversation,
   89:     handleUpdateName,
   90:     handleUpdateMessagePermission,
   91:     handleAddParticipants,
   92:     handleRemoveMember,
   93:     handleGrantRole,
   94:     handleRevokeRole,
   95:     handleMuteMember,
   96:     handleUnmuteMember,
   97:     handleBanMember,
   98:     handleUnbanMember,
   99:     handleTransferOwnership,
  100:     handlePinConversation,
  101:     handleMuteConversationByUser,
  102:     handleArchiveConversation,
  103:   } = useChat(notifyError);
  104: 
  105:   // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  106:   // LOAD
  107:   // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  108:   useEffect(() => {
  109:     if (!open || !isAuthenticated) return;
  110:     void fetchConversations();
  111:   }, [open, isAuthenticated, fetchConversations]);
  112: 
  113:   // BE chưa có topic tin nhắn theo từng user → đồng bộ nhẹ danh sách để
  114:   // badge "chưa đọc" không bị cũ khi widget đóng.
  115:   useEffect(() => {
  116:     if (!isAuthenticated) return;
  117:     const timer = setInterval(() => {
  118:       void fetchConversations();
  119:     }, 60_000);
  120:     return () => clearInterval(timer);
  121:   }, [isAuthenticated, fetchConversations]);
  122: 
  123:   // Chỉ báo "mất kết nối" khi WebSocket không lên sau 2.5s (tránh nhấp nháy)
  124:   useEffect(() => {
  125:     if (connected) return;
  126:     const timer = setTimeout(() => setOffline(true), 2500);
  127:     return () => clearTimeout(timer);
  128:   }, [connected]);
  129: 
  130:   // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  131:   // COMPUTE
  132:   // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  133:   const myRole = currentParticipant?.roleInConversation ?? null;
  134:   const canManage = myRole === 'OWNER' || myRole === 'ADMIN';
  135:   const canModerate = canManage || myRole === 'MODERATOR';
  136:   const isOwner = myRole === 'OWNER';
  137:   const isGroup = activeConversation
  138:     ? activeConversation.conversationType !== 'PRIVATE'
  139:     : false;
  140: 
  141:   const filterByTab = useCallback(
  142:     (archived: boolean) => {
  143:       const kw = searchTerm.trim().toLowerCase();
  144:       return conversations
  145:         .filter((c) => Boolean(c.isArchived) === archived)
  146:         .filter((c) => {
  147:           if (!kw) return true;
  148:           const name = (c.name || '').toLowerCase();
  149:           const preview = (c.lastMessagePreview || '').toLowerCase();
  150:           const people = (c.participants || [])
  151:             .map((p) => `${p.fullName || ''} ${p.username || ''}`)
  152:             .join(' ')
  153:             .toLowerCase();
  154:           return name.includes(kw) || preview.includes(kw) || people.includes(kw);
  155:         })
  156:         .sort((a, b) => Number(!!b.isPinned) - Number(!!a.isPinned));
  157:     },
  158:     [conversations, searchTerm],
  159:   );
  160: 
  161:   const activeList = useMemo(() => filterByTab(false), [filterByTab]);
  162:   const archivedList = useMemo(() => filterByTab(true), [filterByTab]);
  163: 
  164:   const inputDisabled = useMemo(() => {
  165:     if (!activeConversation) return false;
  166:     if (currentParticipant?.chatStatus === 'BANNED') return true;
  167:     if (currentParticipant?.chatStatus === 'MUTED') {
  168:       const until = currentParticipant.mutedUntil;
  169:       if (!until) return true;
  170:       // BE chỉ tự mở mute khi user gửi tin → cần so với thời điểm hiện tại để mở khoá composer
  171:       // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/purity
  172:       if (new Date(until).getTime() > Date.now()) return true;
  173:     }
  174:     if (activeConversation.messagePermission === 'READ_ONLY') return true;
  175:     if (activeConversation.messagePermission === 'ADMIN_ONLY' && !canModerate) return true;
  176:     return false;
  177:   }, [activeConversation, currentParticipant, canModerate]);
  178: 
  179:   const inputDisabledReason = useMemo(() => {
  180:     if (!inputDisabled || !activeConversation) return undefined;
  181:     if (currentParticipant?.chatStatus === 'BANNED') {
  182:       return `Bạn đã bị cấm chat${
  183:         currentParticipant.banReason ? `: ${currentParticipant.banReason}` : ''
  184:       }`;
  185:     }
  186:     if (currentParticipant?.chatStatus === 'MUTED') {
  187:       const until = currentParticipant.mutedUntil
  188:         ? new Date(currentParticipant.mutedUntil).toLocaleString('vi-VN')
  189:         : 'vĩnh viễn';
  190:       return `Bạn đang bị mute đến ${until}`;
  191:     }
  192:     if (activeConversation.messagePermission === 'READ_ONLY') {
  193:       return 'Hội thoại đang ở chế độ chỉ đọc';
  194:     }
  195:     if (activeConversation.messagePermission === 'ADMIN_ONLY') {
  196:       return 'Chỉ quản trị viên mới được gửi tin nhắn';
  197:     }
  198:     return undefined;
  199:   }, [inputDisabled, currentParticipant, activeConversation]);
  200: 
  201:   // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  202:   // NAVIGATION

  204:   const openConversation = (conv: ConversationResponse) => {
  205:     setView('chat');

  207:     void selectConversation(conv);
  208:   };
  209: 
  210:   const backToList = () => {

  212:     setReplyingTo(null);

  214:   };
  215:

  218:     setView('list');
  219:     setTab('conversations');

  221:     setReplyingTo(null);

  224:

  229:       await startPrivateConversation(u.accountId);
  230:       setTab('conversations');
  231:       setView('chat');
  232:       setReplyingTo(null);

  234:       /* đã toast */
  235:     } finally {
  236:       setStartingId(null);

  239:

  243:   const handleSend = async (content: string) => {

  245:     setReplyingTo(null);
  246:   };
  247:

  251:     } catch {
  252:       /* đã toast */
  253:     }
  254:   };
  255:

  258:     try {
  259:       await deleteMessage(id);
  260:     } catch {
  261:       /* đã toast */
  262:     }
  263:   };
  264:

  268:   const handlePin = async (conv: ConversationResponse) => {

  275:

  278:       await handleMuteConversationByUser(conv.id, !conv.isMutedByUser);
  279:     } catch {
  280:       /* đã toast */
  281:     }

  283:

  288:       /* đã toast */

  291: 
  292:   // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  293:   // MEMBERS
  294:   // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  295:   const handleGrant = async (accountId: number, role: ParticipantRole) => {
  296:     try {
  297:       await handleGrantRole(accountId, role);
  298:       toast.success(role === 'ADMIN' ? 'Đã cấp quyền ADMIN' : 'Đã cấp quyền MODERATOR');
  299:     } catch {
  300:       /* đã toast */
  301:     }
  302:   };
  303: 
  304:   const handleRevoke = async (accountId: number) => {
  305:     try {
  306:       await handleRevokeRole(accountId);
  307:       toast.success('Đã thu hồi quyền');
  308:     } catch {
  309:       /* đã toast */
  310:     }
  311:   };
  312: 
  313:   const handleMute = async (accountId: number, duration: MuteDuration) => {
  314:     try {
  315:       await handleMuteMember(accountId, muteUntilFor(duration), MUTE_REASON[duration]);
  316:       toast.success(duration === 'forever' ? 'Đã mute vĩnh viễn' : `Đã mute ${duration}`);
  317:     } catch {
  318:       /* đã toast */
  319:     }
  320:   };
  321: 
  322:   const handleUnmute = async (accountId: number) => {
  323:     try {
  324:       await handleUnmuteMember(accountId);
  325:       toast.success('Đã bỏ mute');
  326:     } catch {
  327:       /* đã toast */
  328:     }
  329:   };
  330: 
  331:   const handleBan = async (accountId: number) => {
  332:     if (!window.confirm('Cấm thành viên này gửi tin nhắn?')) return;
  333:     try {
  334:       await handleBanMember(accountId, 'Vi phạm nội quy');
  335:       toast.success('Đã cấm thành viên');
  336:     } catch {
  337:       /* đã toast */
  338:     }
  339:   };
  340: 
  341:   const handleUnban = async (accountId: number) => {
  342:     try {
  343:       await handleUnbanMember(accountId);
  344:       toast.success('Đã bỏ cấm');
  345:     } catch {
  346:       /* đã toast */
  347:     }
  348:   };
  349: 
  350:   const handleKick = async (accountId: number) => {
  351:     if (!window.confirm('Xoá thành viên này khỏi nhóm?')) return;
  352:     try {
  353:       await handleRemoveMember(accountId);
  354:       toast.success('Đã xoá khỏi nhóm');
  355:     } catch {
  356:       /* đã toast */
  357:     }
  358:   };
  359: 
  360:   const handleTransferOwner = async (accountId: number) => {
  361:     if (
  362:       !window.confirm('Chuyển quyền trưởng nhóm cho thành viên này? Bạn sẽ trở thành ADMIN.')
  363:     ) {
  364:       return;
  365:     }
  366:     try {
  367:       await handleTransferOwnership(accountId);
  368:       toast.success('Đã chuyển quyền trưởng nhóm');
  369:     } catch {
  370:       /* đã toast */
  371:     }
  372:   };
  373: 
  374:   const handleStartPrivate = async (accountId: number) => {
  375:     try {
  376:       await startPrivateConversation(accountId);
  377:       setView('chat');
  378:       setReplyingTo(null);
  379:       setTab('conversations');
  380:     } catch {
  381:       /* đã toast */
  382:     }
  383:   };
  384: 
  385:   const handleAddMembers = async (accountIds: number[]) => {
  386:     await handleAddParticipants(accountIds);
  387:     toast.success('Đã thêm thành viên');
  388:   };
  389: 
  390:   if (!isAuthenticated) return null;
  391: 
  392:   const tabClass = (active: boolean) =>
  393:     `flex h-7 flex-1 items-center justify-center gap-1.5 rounded-lg border-0 bg-transparent text-[11.5px] font-semibold transition-all ${
  394:       active
  395:         ? 'bg-white text-brand-700 shadow-[0_1px_3px_rgba(15,23,42,0.12)]'
  396:         : 'text-slate-500 hover:text-slate-700'
  397:     }`;
  398: 
  399:   // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  400:   // RENDER
  401:   // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  402:   return (
  403:     <>
  404:       <button
  405:         type="button"
  406:         onClick={() => setOpen((prev) => !prev)}
  407:         className="relative inline-flex h-10 w-10 items-center justify-center rounded-[10px] border-0 bg-transparent text-slate-600 transition-colors hover:bg-slate-100 hover:text-brand-700"
  408:         title="Tin nhắn"
  409:         aria-label="Tin nhắn"
  410:       >
  411:         <FaComments size={18} />
  412:         {totalUnread > 0 && (
  413:           <span className="absolute right-1 top-1 min-w-4 rounded-full bg-rose-500 px-1 text-center text-[10px] font-bold leading-4 text-white shadow-[0_0_0_2px_#fff]">
  414:             {totalUnread > 99 ? '99+' : totalUnread}
  415:           </span>
  416:         )}
  417:       </button>
  418: 
  419:       {open && (
  420:         <div className="fixed bottom-5 right-5 z-[1200] flex h-[600px] w-[400px] max-h-[calc(100vh-24px)] max-w-[calc(100vw-24px)] flex-col overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white shadow-[0_24px_48px_-18px_rgba(15,23,42,0.30),0_2px_10px_rgba(15,23,42,0.06)] animate-chat-in">
  421:           {/* ════════ HEADER ════════ */}
  422:           {view === 'list' && (
  423:             <ChatWindowHeader
  424:               title="Tin nhắn"
  425:               subtitle={`${activeList.length} hội thoại${
  426:                 archivedList.length > 0 ? ` · ${archivedList.length} lưu trữ` : ''
  427:               }`}
  428:               onClose={handleClose}
  429:             />
  430:           )}
  431: 
  432:           {view === 'chat' && activeConversation && (
  433:             <ChatWindowHeader
  434:               title={getConversationTitle(activeConversation, currentUserId)}
  435:               subtitle={
  436:                 isGroup
  437:                   ? `${participants.length} thành viên · ${messages.length} tin nhắn`
  438:                   : `Trò chuyện riêng · ${messages.length} tin nhắn`
  439:               }
  440:               showBack
  441:               onBack={backToList}
  442:               onClose={handleClose}
  443:               avatarUrl={getConversationAvatar(activeConversation, currentUserId)}
  444:               avatarEmoji={getConvIcon(activeConversation.conversationType)}
  445:               isGroup={isGroup}
  446:               canManage={canManage}
  447:               messagePermission={
  448:                 (activeConversation.messagePermission as MessagePermission) || 'EVERYONE'
  449:               }
  450:               onShowMembers={() => setView('members')}
  451:               onAddMembers={isGroup && canManage ? () => setView('addMembers') : undefined}
  452:               onUpdateName={isGroup && canManage ? handleUpdateName : undefined}
  453:               onUpdatePermission={
  454:                 isGroup && canManage ? handleUpdateMessagePermission : undefined
  455:               }
  456:             />
  457:           )}
  458: 
  459:           {view === 'members' && activeConversation && (
  460:             <ChatWindowHeader
  461:               title="Thành viên"
  462:               subtitle={`${participants.length} người`}
  463:               showBack
  464:               onBack={() => setView('chat')}
  465:               onClose={handleClose}
  466:             />
  467:           )}
  468: 
  469:           {view === 'addMembers' && activeConversation && (
  470:             <ChatWindowHeader
  471:               title="Thêm thành viên"
  472:               subtitle={getConversationTitle(activeConversation, currentUserId)}
  473:               showBack
  474:               onBack={() => setView('members')}
  475:               onClose={handleClose}
  476:             />
  477:           )}
  478: 
  479:           {view === 'createGroup' && (
  480:             <ChatWindowHeader
  481:               title="Tạo nhóm chat"
  482:               subtitle="Nhập tên và chọn thành viên"
  483:               showBack
  484:               onBack={() => setView('list')}
  485:               onClose={handleClose}
  486:             />
  487:           )}
  488: 
  489:           {/* ════════ MẤT KẾT NỐI ════════ */}
  490:           {!connected && offline && (
  491:             <div className="flex shrink-0 items-center gap-1.5 border-b border-amber-200 bg-amber-50 px-3 py-1 text-[10.5px] font-semibold text-amber-700">
  492:               <span className="inline-block h-1.5 w-1.5 rounded-full bg-amber-500" />
  493:               Mất kết nối realtime — đang kết nối lại...
  494:             </div>
  495:           )}
  496: 
  497:           {/* ════════ LIST VIEW ════════ */}
  498:           {view === 'list' && (
  499:             <div className="flex min-h-0 flex-1 flex-col bg-white">
  500:               <div className="shrink-0 px-3 pb-0.5 pt-2.5">
  501:                 <div className="flex gap-0.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-100 p-[3px]">
  502:                   <button
  503:                     type="button"
  504:                     onClick={() => setTab('conversations')}
  505:                     className={tabClass(tab === 'conversations')}
  506:                   >
  507:                     <FaComments size={10} /> Đoạn chat
  508:                   </button>
  509:                   <button
  510:                     type="button"
  511:                     onClick={() => setTab('archived')}
  512:                     className={tabClass(tab === 'archived')}
  513:                   >
  514:                     <FaArchive size={10} /> Lưu trữ
  515:                     {archivedList.length > 0 && (
  516:                       <span className="rounded-full bg-slate-200 px-1 text-[9.5px] font-bold leading-[14px] text-slate-500">
  517:                         {archivedList.length}
  518:                       </span>
  519:                     )}
  520:                   </button>
  521:                   <button
  522:                     type="button"
  523:                     onClick={() => setTab('new')}
  524:                     className={tabClass(tab === 'new')}
  525:                   >
  526:                     <FaUsers size={10} /> Mới
  527:                   </button>
  528:                 </div>
  529:               </div>
  530: 
  531:               {tab === 'new' ? (
  532:                 <NewChatView
  533:                   onStartChat={handleStartChat}
  534:                   onCreateGroup={() => setView('createGroup')}
  535:                   startingId={startingId}
  536:                 />
  537:               ) : (
  538:                 <div className="flex min-h-0 flex-1 flex-col">
  539:                   <ConversationList
  540:                     conversations={tab === 'archived' ? archivedList : activeList}
  541:                     currentUserId={currentUserId}
  542:                     loading={loading}
  543:                     searchTerm={searchTerm}
  544:                     onSearchChange={setSearchTerm}
  545:                     emptyTitle={
  546:                       tab === 'archived'
  547:                         ? 'Không có hội thoại lưu trữ'
  548:                         : searchTerm
  549:                           ? 'Không tìm thấy kết quả'
  550:                           : 'Chưa có cuộc trò chuyện nào'
  551:                     }
  552:                     emptyHint={
  553:                       tab === 'archived'
  554:                         ? 'Hội thoại bạn lưu trữ sẽ xuất hiện ở đây'
  555:                         : searchTerm
  556:                           ? 'Thử từ khoá khác'
  557:                           : 'Bắt đầu trò chuyện với đồng nghiệp'
  558:                     }
  559:                     emptyActionLabel={
  560:                       tab === 'conversations' && !searchTerm
  561:                         ? '+ Bắt đầu trò chuyện'
  562:                         : undefined
  563:                     }
  564:                     onEmptyAction={() => setTab('new')}
  565:                     onOpen={openConversation}
  566:                     onPin={handlePin}
  567:                     onToggleMute={handleToggleMute}
  568:                     onToggleArchive={handleToggleArchive}
  569:                   />
  570:                 </div>
  571:               )}
  572:             </div>
  573:           )}
  574: 
  575:           {/* ════════ CHAT VIEW ════════ */}
  576:           {view === 'chat' && activeConversation && (
  577:             <>
  578:               <ChatMessageList
  579:                 messages={messages}
  580:                 currentUserId={currentUserId}
  581:                 loading={loadingMessages}
  582:                 hasMore={hasMoreMessages}
  583:                 loadingMore={loadingMore}
  584:                 onLoadMore={loadOlderMessages}
  585:                 showSender={isGroup}
  586:                 canModerate={canModerate}
  587:                 canEdit={!inputDisabled}
  588:                 participants={participants}
  589:                 readReceipts={readReceipts}
  590:                 onReply={setReplyingTo}
  591:                 onEdit={handleEditMessage}
  592:                 onDelete={handleDeleteMessage}
  593:               />
  594:               <ChatMessageInput
  595:                 onSend={handleSend}
  596:                 disabled={inputDisabled}
  597:                 disabledReason={inputDisabledReason}
  598:                 replyingTo={replyingTo}
  599:                 onCancelReply={() => setReplyingTo(null)}
  600:               />
  601:             </>
  602:           )}
  603: 
  604:           {/* ════════ MEMBERS VIEW ════════ */}
  605:           {view === 'members' && activeConversation && (
  606:             <MembersView
  607:               participants={participants}
  608:               currentUserId={currentUserId}
  609:               isGroup={isGroup}
  610:               canManage={canManage}
  611:               canModerate={canModerate}
  612:               isOwner={isOwner}
  613:               onGrantRole={handleGrant}
  614:               onRevokeRole={handleRevoke}
  615:               onMute={handleMute}
  616:               onUnmute={handleUnmute}
  617:               onBan={handleBan}
  618:               onUnban={handleUnban}
  619:               onKick={handleKick}
  620:               onTransferOwner={handleTransferOwner}
  621:               onStartPrivateChat={handleStartPrivate}
  622:               onAddMembers={() => setView('addMembers')}
  623:             />
  624:           )}
  625: 
  626:           {/* ════════ ADD MEMBERS VIEW ════════ */}
  627:           {view === 'addMembers' && activeConversation && (
  628:             <AddMembersView
  629:               participants={participants}
  630:               onAdd={handleAddMembers}
  631:               onDone={() => setView('members')}
  632:             />
  633:           )}
  634: 
  635:           {/* ════════ CREATE GROUP VIEW ════════ */}
  636:           {view === 'createGroup' && (
  637:             <CreateGroupView
  638:               onCreate={createGroupConversation}
  639:               onCreated={() => {
  640:                 setTab('conversations');
  641:                 setView('chat');
  642:                 setReplyingTo(null);
  643:               }}
  644:             />
  645:           )}
  646:         </div>
  647:       )}
  648:     </>
  649:   );
  650: };
  651: 
  652: export default ChatWidget;

// ============================================================
// FILE: ./src/components/chat/ChatWindowHeader.tsx
// ============================================================
    1: // src/components/chat/ChatWindowHeader.tsx
    2: import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
    3: import type { MouseEvent } from 'react';
    4: import {
    5:   FaArrowLeft,
    6:   FaCheck,
    7:   FaEllipsisV,
    8:   FaLock,
    9:   FaTimes,
   10:   FaUnlock,
   11:   FaUserPlus,
   12:   FaUsers,
   13: } from 'react-icons/fa';
   14: import type { MessagePermission } from '@/services/types/chat';
   15: import { anchorMenu } from './chatHelpers';
   16: 
   17: interface Props {
   18:   title: string;
   19:   subtitle?: string;
   20:   showBack?: boolean;
   21:   onBack?: () => void;
   22:   onClose?: () => void;
   23:   /** Ảnh / emoji đại diện hội thoại */
   24:   avatarUrl?: string | null;
   25:   avatarEmoji?: string | null;
   26:   /** Chỉ hiện menu "..." khi có ít nhất 1 handler */
   27:   isGroup?: boolean;
   28:   canManage?: boolean;
   29:   messagePermission?: MessagePermission;
   30:   onShowMembers?: () => void;
   31:   onAddMembers?: () => void;
   32:   onUpdateName?: (newName: string) => void;
   33:   onUpdatePermission?: (perm: MessagePermission) => void;
   34: }
   35: 
   36: const iconBtn =
   37:   'inline-flex h-[30px] w-[30px] shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-[9px] ' +
   38:   'border-0 bg-transparent text-white transition-colors hover:bg-white/20 active:bg-white/25';
   39: 
   40: const menuItem =
   41:   'flex w-full items-center gap-2 rounded-[7px] border-0 bg-transparent px-2.5 py-2 ' +
   42:   'text-left text-xs font-semibold text-slate-600 transition-colors hover:bg-slate-50';
   43: 
   44: const ChatWindowHeader = ({
   45:   title,
   46:   subtitle,
   47:   showBack = false,
   48:   onBack,
   49:   onClose,
   50:   avatarUrl,
   51:   avatarEmoji,
   52:   isGroup = false,
   53:   canManage = false,
   54:   messagePermission = 'EVERYONE',
   55:   onShowMembers,
   56:   onAddMembers,
   57:   onUpdateName,
   58:   onUpdatePermission,
   59: }: Props) => {
   60:   const [menu, setMenu] = useState<{ top: number; left: number } | null>(null);
   61:   const [editingName, setEditingName] = useState(false);
   62:   const [nameInput, setNameInput] = useState('');
   63:   const inputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);
   64: 
   65:   useEffect(() => {
   66:     if (!editingName) return;
   67:     const timer = setTimeout(() => inputRef.current?.focus(), 50);
   68:     return () => clearTimeout(timer);
   69:   }, [editingName]);
   70: 
   71:   // Đổi view/tiêu đề thì đóng menu + huỷ sửa tên
   72:   const canRename = isGroup && canManage && Boolean(onUpdateName);
   73:   const hasMenu = Boolean(onShowMembers || onAddMembers || onUpdatePermission);
   74:   const isAdminsOnly = messagePermission === 'ADMIN_ONLY';
   75:   const isReadOnly = messagePermission === 'READ_ONLY';
   76: 
   77:   const handleSaveName = () => {
   78:     const trimmed = nameInput.trim();
   79:     if (trimmed && trimmed !== title) onUpdateName?.(trimmed);
   80:     setEditingName(false);
   81:   };
   82: 
   83:   const openMenu = (e: MouseEvent<HTMLButtonElement>) => {
   84:     if (menu) {
   85:       setMenu(null);
   86:       return;
   87:     }
   88:     setMenu(anchorMenu(e.currentTarget, 196, 232));
   89:   };
   90: 
   91:   return (
   92:     <>
   93:       <div className="flex h-14 shrink-0 items-center gap-1.5 bg-brand px-2 text-white">
   94:         <div className="flex min-w-0 flex-1 items-center gap-2.5">
   95:           {showBack && onBack && (
   96:             <button type="button" onClick={onBack} className={iconBtn} title="Quay lại">
   97:               <FaArrowLeft size={13} />
   98:             </button>
   99:           )}
  100: 
  101:           {(avatarUrl || avatarEmoji) && (
  102:             <div className="flex h-8 w-8 shrink-0 items-center justify-center overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-white/25 text-[15px]">
  103:               {avatarUrl ? (
  104:                 <img src={avatarUrl} alt="" className="h-full w-full object-cover" />
  105:               ) : (
  106:                 avatarEmoji
  107:               )}
  108:             </div>
  109:           )}
  110: 
  111:           <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
  112:             {editingName ? (
  113:               <div className="flex items-center gap-1">
  114:                 <input
  115:                   ref={inputRef}
  116:                   type="text"
  117:                   value={nameInput}
  118:                   onChange={(e) => setNameInput(e.target.value)}
  119:                   onKeyDown={(e) => {
  120:                     if (e.key === 'Enter') handleSaveName();
  121:                     if (e.key === 'Escape') setEditingName(false);
  122:                   }}
  123:                   maxLength={255}
  124:                   className="h-7 min-w-0 flex-1 rounded-[7px] border border-white/70 bg-white px-2 text-[12.5px] font-semibold text-slate-900 outline-none"
  125:                 />
  126:                 <button
  127:                   type="button"
  128:                   onClick={handleSaveName}
  129:                   className={iconBtn}
  130:                   title="Lưu tên"
  131:                 >
  132:                   <FaCheck size={11} />
  133:                 </button>
  134:                 <button
  135:                   type="button"
  136:                   onClick={() => setEditingName(false)}
  137:                   className={iconBtn}
  138:                   title="Huỷ"
  139:                 >
  140:                   <FaTimes size={11} />
  141:                 </button>
  142:               </div>
  143:             ) : (
  144:               <div
  145:                 className={`flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[13.5px] font-bold leading-[18px] ${
  146:                   canRename ? 'cursor-pointer' : ''
  147:                 }`}
  148:                 onDoubleClick={() => {
  149:                   if (!canRename) return;
  150:                   setNameInput(title);
  151:                   setEditingName(true);
  152:                 }}
  153:                 title={canRename ? 'Nháy đúp để đổi tên nhóm' : undefined}
  154:               >
  155:                 <span className="truncate">{title}</span>
  156:                 {isAdminsOnly && (
  157:                   <FaLock size={10} className="shrink-0 text-amber-300" title="Chỉ admin gửi" />
  158:                 )}
  159:                 {isReadOnly && (
  160:                   <span className="shrink-0 rounded-full bg-white/25 px-1.5 text-[9px] font-bold leading-[14px]">
  161:                     chỉ đọc
  162:                   </span>
  163:                 )}
  164:               </div>
  165:             )}
  166:             {subtitle && (
  167:               <div className="truncate text-[11px] leading-[15px] text-white/80">{subtitle}</div>
  168:             )}
  169:           </div>
  170:         </div>
  171: 
  172:         <div className="flex shrink-0 items-center gap-0.5">
  173:           {hasMenu && (
  174:             <button
  175:               type="button"
  176:               onClick={openMenu}
  177:               className={iconBtn}
  178:               title="Tuỳ chọn hội thoại"
  179:             >
  180:               <FaEllipsisV size={13} />
  181:             </button>
  182:           )}
  183:           {onClose && (
  184:             <button type="button" onClick={onClose} className={iconBtn} title="Đóng">
  185:               <FaTimes size={15} />
  186:             </button>
  187:           )}
  188:         </div>
  189:       </div>
  190: 
  191:       {menu && (
  192:         <>
  193:           <div className="fixed inset-0 z-[1250]" onClick={() => setMenu(null)} />
  194:           <div
  195:             className="fixed z-[1300] min-w-[196px] rounded-xl border border-slate-100 bg-white p-1 shadow-[0_12px_28px_-10px_rgba(15,23,42,0.26),0_2px_6px_rgba(15,23,42,0.06)]"
  196:             style={{ top: menu.top, left: menu.left }}
  197:           >
  198:             {onShowMembers && (
  199:               <button
  200:                 type="button"
  201:                 onClick={() => {
  202:                   setMenu(null);
  203:                   onShowMembers();
  204:                 }}
  205:                 className={menuItem}
  206:               >
  207:                 <FaUsers size={12} className="text-slate-400" />
  208:                 Quản lý thành viên
  209:               </button>
  210:             )}
  211:             {onAddMembers && (
  212:               <button
  213:                 type="button"
  214:                 onClick={() => {
  215:                   setMenu(null);
  216:                   onAddMembers();
  217:                 }}
  218:                 className={menuItem}
  219:               >
  220:                 <FaUserPlus size={12} className="text-slate-400" />
  221:                 Thêm thành viên
  222:               </button>
  223:             )}
  224:             {onUpdatePermission && isGroup && canManage && (
  225:               <>
  226:                 <div className="my-1 h-px bg-slate-100" />
  227:                 <button
  228:                   type="button"
  229:                   onClick={() => {
  230:                     setMenu(null);
  231:                     onUpdatePermission(isAdminsOnly ? 'EVERYONE' : 'ADMIN_ONLY');
  232:                   }}
  233:                   className={menuItem}
  234:                 >
  235:                   {isAdminsOnly ? (
  236:                     <>
  237:                       <FaUnlock size={12} className="text-emerald-500" />
  238:                       Cho mọi người gửi tin
  239:                     </>
  240:                   ) : (
  241:                     <>
  242:                       <FaLock size={12} className="text-amber-500" />
  243:                       Chỉ admin được gửi
  244:                     </>
  245:                   )}
  246:                 </button>
  247:                 <button
  248:                   type="button"
  249:                   onClick={() => {
  250:                     setMenu(null);
  251:                     onUpdatePermission(isReadOnly ? 'EVERYONE' : 'READ_ONLY');
  252:                   }}
  253:                   className={menuItem}
  254:                 >
  255:                   <FaLock size={12} className="text-rose-500" />
  256:                   {isReadOnly ? 'Mở lại quyền gửi tin' : 'Chuyển sang chỉ đọc'}
  257:                 </button>
  258:               </>
  259:             )}
  260:           </div>
  261:         </>
  262:       )}
  263:     </>
