// FILE: ./src/components/chat/CreateGroupView.tsx
// ============================================================
    1: // src/components/chat/CreateGroupView.tsx
    2: import { useState } from 'react';
    3: import { FaSpinner, FaTimes, FaUsers } from 'react-icons/fa';
    4: import type { ConversationResponse, UserSearchResponse } from '@/services/types/chat';
    5: import UserPicker from './UserPicker';
    6: 
    7: interface Props {
    8:   /** Gọi API tạo nhóm (GROUP) — thường là useChat().createGroupConversation */
    9:   onCreate: (name: string, participantIds: number[]) => Promise<ConversationResponse>;
   10:   /** Điều hướng sau khi tạo xong */
   11:   onCreated: (conversation: ConversationResponse) => void;
   12: }
   13: 
   14: /**
   15:  * View tạo nhóm chat: nhập tên + chọn thành viên.
   16:  * BE: POST /api/chat/conversations { conversationType: 'GROUP', name, participantIds }
   17:  */
   18: const CreateGroupView = ({ onCreate, onCreated }: Props) => {
   19:   const [name, setName] = useState('');
   20:   const [selected, setSelected] = useState<UserSearchResponse[]>([]);
   21:   const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);
   22:   const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
   23: 
   24:   const selectedIds = selected.map((u) => u.accountId);
   25:   const canSubmit = name.trim().length > 0 && selected.length > 0 && !submitting;
   26: 
   27:   const toggle = (u: UserSearchResponse) => {
   28:     setError(null);
   29:     setSelected((prev) =>
   30:       prev.some((x) => x.accountId === u.accountId)
   31:         ? prev.filter((x) => x.accountId !== u.accountId)
   32:         : [...prev, u],
   33:     );
   34:   };
   35: 
   36:   const handleSubmit = async () => {
   37:     if (!canSubmit) {
   38:       setError(
   39:         name.trim().length === 0
   40:           ? 'Vui lòng nhập tên nhóm'
   41:           : 'Vui lòng chọn ít nhất 1 thành viên',
   42:       );
   43:       return;
   44:     }
   45:     setSubmitting(true);
   46:     setError(null);
   47:     try {
   48:       const conv = await onCreate(name.trim(), selectedIds);
   49:       setName('');
   50:       setSelected([]);
   51:       onCreated(conv);
   52:     } catch (err) {
   53:       setError((err as { message?: string })?.message || 'Không thể tạo nhóm chat');
   54:     } finally {
   55:       setSubmitting(false);
   56:     }
   57:   };
   58: 
   59:   return (
   60:     <div className="flex min-h-0 flex-1 flex-col bg-white">
   61:       <div className="shrink-0 border-b border-slate-100 px-3 pb-2.5 pt-2.5">
   62:         <label className="mb-1 block text-[10.5px] font-bold text-slate-500">
   63:           Tên nhóm <span className="text-rose-500">*</span>
   64:         </label>
   65:         <div className="relative">
   66:           <FaUsers
   67:             size={11}
   68:             className="pointer-events-none absolute left-2.5 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-slate-400"
   69:           />
   70:           <input
   71:             type="text"
   72:             value={name}
   73:             onChange={(e) => {
   74:               setName(e.target.value);
   75:               setError(null);
   76:             }}
   77:             maxLength={255}
   78:             autoFocus
   79:             placeholder="VD: Nhóm K12 - Luyện đề"
   80:             className="h-9 w-full rounded-[10px] border border-slate-200 bg-slate-50 pl-8 pr-2.5 text-xs text-slate-700 outline-none transition placeholder:text-slate-400 focus:border-brand focus:bg-white focus:ring-[3px] focus:ring-brand/15"
   81:           />
   82:         </div>
   83: 
   84:         {selected.length > 0 && (
   85:           <div className="mt-2 flex max-h-[76px] flex-wrap gap-1 overflow-y-auto">
   86:             <span className="self-center text-[10.5px] font-semibold text-slate-500">
   87:               Đã chọn {selected.length}:
   88:             </span>
   89:             {selected.map((u) => (
   90:               <span
   91:                 key={u.accountId}
   92:                 className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded-full bg-brand/10 py-0.5 pl-2 pr-1 text-[10.5px] font-semibold leading-4 text-brand-700"
   93:               >
   94:                 <span className="max-w-[120px] truncate">
   95:                   {u.fullName || u.username || `User #${u.accountId}`}
   96:                 </span>
   97:                 <button
   98:                   type="button"
   99:                   onClick={() =>
  100:                     setSelected((prev) => prev.filter((x) => x.accountId !== u.accountId))
  101:                   }
  102:                   className="rounded-full border-0 bg-transparent p-0.5 transition-colors hover:bg-brand/20"
  103:                   title="Bỏ chọn"
  104:                 >
  105:                   <FaTimes size={8} />
  106:                 </button>
  107:               </span>
  108:             ))}
  109:           </div>
  110:         )}
  111:       </div>
  112: 
  113:       <UserPicker
  114:         selectedIds={selectedIds}
  115:         onToggle={toggle}
  116:         placeholder="Tìm người để thêm vào nhóm..."
  117:         idleHint="Nhập ít nhất 2 ký tự để tìm thành viên"
  118:       />
  119: 
  120:       <div className="shrink-0 border-t border-slate-200 px-3 pb-2.5 pt-2">
  121:         {error && <div className="mb-1.5 text-[11px] font-semibold text-rose-500">{error}</div>}
  122:         <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
  123:           <button
  124:             type="button"
  125:             onClick={handleSubmit}
  126:             disabled={!canSubmit}
  127:             className="inline-flex h-9 flex-1 items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl bg-brand px-3 text-xs font-bold text-white transition-colors hover:bg-brand-600 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-45"
  128:           >
  129:             {submitting ? (
  130:               <FaSpinner className="animate-spin" size={12} />
  131:             ) : (
  132:               <FaUsers size={12} />
  133:             )}
  134:             Tạo nhóm
  135:           </button>
  136:           <span className="shrink-0 whitespace-nowrap text-[10.5px] text-slate-400">
  137:             {selected.length} thành viên
  138:           </span>
  139:         </div>
  140:       </div>
  141:     </div>
  142:   );
  143: };
  144: 
  145: export default CreateGroupView;

// ============================================================
// FILE: ./src/components/chat/MembersView.tsx
// ============================================================
    1: // src/components/chat/MembersView.tsx
    2: import { useMemo, useState } from 'react';
    3: import type { MouseEvent } from 'react';
    4: import {
    5:   FaArrowUp,
    6:   FaBan,
    7:   FaCheck,
    8:   FaComments,
    9:   FaCrown,
   10:   FaEllipsisV,
   11:   FaMicrophoneSlash,
   12:   FaSearch,
   13:   FaTrash,
   14:   FaUserMinus,
   15:   FaUserPlus,
   16:   FaUserShield,
   17:   FaUsers,
   18: } from 'react-icons/fa';
   19: import type { ParticipantResponse, ParticipantRole } from '@/services/types/chat';
   20: import { anchorMenu } from './chatHelpers';
   21: 
   22: export type MuteDuration = '1h' | '24h' | 'forever';
   23: 
   24: interface Props {
   25:   participants: ParticipantResponse[];
   26:   currentUserId?: number;
   27:   isGroup: boolean;
   28:   canManage: boolean;
   29:   canModerate: boolean;
   30:   isOwner: boolean;
   31:   onGrantRole: (accountId: number, role: ParticipantRole) => void;
   32:   onRevokeRole: (accountId: number) => void;
   33:   onMute: (accountId: number, duration: MuteDuration) => void;
   34:   onUnmute: (accountId: number) => void;
   35:   onBan: (accountId: number) => void;
   36:   onUnban: (accountId: number) => void;
   37:   onKick: (accountId: number) => void;
   38:   onTransferOwner: (accountId: number) => void;
   39:   onStartPrivateChat: (accountId: number) => void;
   40:   onAddMembers: () => void;
   41: }
   42: 
   43: const roleLabel: Record<ParticipantRole, string> = {
   44:   OWNER: 'Trưởng nhóm',
   45:   ADMIN: 'Quản trị',
   46:   MODERATOR: 'Điều hành',
   47:   MEMBER: 'Thành viên',
   48: };
   49: 
   50: const roleTagClass: Record<ParticipantRole, string> = {
   51:   OWNER: 'bg-amber-100 text-amber-700',
   52:   ADMIN: 'bg-blue-100 text-blue-700',
   53:   MODERATOR: 'bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-700',
   54:   MEMBER: 'bg-slate-100 text-slate-500',
   55: };
   56: 
   57: const formatMutedUntil = (until?: string | null) => {
   58:   if (!until) return 'vĩnh viễn';
   59:   const d = new Date(until);
   60:   if (Number.isNaN(d.getTime())) return 'vĩnh viễn';
   61:   return d.toLocaleString('vi-VN', {
   62:     hour: '2-digit',
   63:     minute: '2-digit',
   64:     day: '2-digit',
   65:     month: '2-digit',
   66:   });
   67: };
   68: 
   69: const tag = 'rounded-full px-1.5 py-px text-[9.5px] font-bold leading-[14px]';
   70: const menuItem =
   71:   'flex w-full items-center gap-2 rounded-[7px] border-0 bg-transparent px-2.5 py-2 text-left text-xs font-semibold transition-colors';
   72: 
   73: const MembersView = ({
   74:   participants,
   75:   currentUserId,
   76:   isGroup,
   77:   canManage,
   78:   canModerate,
   79:   isOwner,
   80:   onGrantRole,
   81:   onRevokeRole,
   82:   onMute,
   83:   onUnmute,
   84:   onBan,
   85:   onUnban,
   86:   onKick,
   87:   onTransferOwner,
   88:   onStartPrivateChat,
   89:   onAddMembers,
   90: }: Props) => {
   91:   const [menu, setMenu] = useState<{ id: number; top: number; left: number } | null>(null);
   92:   const [keyword, setKeyword] = useState('');
   93: 
   94:   const filtered = useMemo(() => {
   95:     const kw = keyword.trim().toLowerCase();
   96:     const order: Record<string, number> = { OWNER: 0, ADMIN: 1, MODERATOR: 2, MEMBER: 3 };
   97:     const sorted = [...participants].sort(
   98:       (a, b) => (order[a.roleInConversation] ?? 9) - (order[b.roleInConversation] ?? 9),
   99:     );
  100:     if (!kw) return sorted;
  101:     return sorted.filter((p) =>
  102:       `${p.fullName || ''} ${p.username || ''}`.toLowerCase().includes(kw),
  103:     );
  104:   }, [participants, keyword]);
  105: 
  106:   const target = filtered.find((p) => p.accountId === menu?.id) ?? null;
  107: 
  108:   /** Được thao tác trên người này (không phải mình, không phải OWNER trừ khi mình là OWNER) */
  109:   const actionable = target
  110:     ? target.accountId !== currentUserId &&
  111:       (target.roleInConversation !== 'OWNER' || isOwner)
  112:     : false;
  113: 
  114:   const openMenu = (e: MouseEvent<HTMLButtonElement>, accountId: number) => {
  115:     if (menu?.id === accountId) {
  116:       setMenu(null);
  117:       return;
  118:     }
  119:     setMenu({ id: accountId, ...anchorMenu(e.currentTarget, 196, 288) });
  120:   };
  121: 
  122:   const close = () => setMenu(null);
  123: 
  124:   return (
  125:     <div className="flex min-h-0 flex-1 flex-col bg-white">
  126:       <div className="shrink-0 border-b border-slate-100 px-3 pb-1.5 pt-2">
  127:         <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
  128:           <div className="relative flex-1">
  129:             <FaSearch
  130:               size={11}
  131:               className="pointer-events-none absolute left-2.5 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-slate-400"
  132:             />
  133:             <input
  134:               type="text"
  135:               value={keyword}
  136:               onChange={(e) => setKeyword(e.target.value)}
  137:               placeholder="Tìm thành viên..."
  138:               className="h-8 w-full rounded-[10px] border border-slate-200 bg-slate-50 pl-8 pr-2.5 text-xs text-slate-700 outline-none transition placeholder:text-slate-400 focus:border-brand focus:bg-white focus:ring-[3px] focus:ring-brand/15"
  139:             />
  140:           </div>
  141:           {isGroup && canManage && (
  142:             <button
  143:               type="button"
  144:               onClick={onAddMembers}
  145:               className="inline-flex h-8 shrink-0 items-center gap-1.5 rounded-[10px] bg-brand px-2.5 text-[11px] font-bold text-white transition-colors hover:bg-brand-600"
  146:             >
  147:               <FaUserPlus size={10} />
  148:               Thêm
  149:             </button>
  150:           )}
  151:         </div>
  152:         <div className="mt-1.5 flex items-center gap-1 text-[10px] font-semibold text-slate-400">
  153:           <FaUsers size={9} />
  154:           {participants.length} thành viên
  155:           {!isGroup && ' · chat riêng'}
  156:         </div>
  157:       </div>
  158: 
  159:       <div className="min-h-0 flex-1 overflow-y-auto" onScroll={close}>
  160:         {filtered.length === 0 ? (
  161:           <div className="flex h-full flex-col items-center justify-center gap-1 text-center">
  162:             <FaUsers className="mb-1 text-[26px] text-slate-300" />
  163:             <div className="text-[12.5px] font-semibold text-slate-500">
  164:               Không tìm thấy thành viên
  165:             </div>
  166:           </div>
  167:         ) : (
  168:           filtered.map((p) => {
  169:             const isSelf = p.accountId === currentUserId;
  170: 
  171:             return (
  172:               <div
  173:                 key={p.accountId}
  174:                 className="flex items-center gap-2.5 border-b border-slate-100 px-3 py-2 transition-colors hover:bg-slate-50"
  175:               >
  176:                 <div className="flex h-9 w-9 shrink-0 items-center justify-center overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-brand/10 text-xs font-bold text-brand">
  177:                   {p.avatarUrl ? (
  178:                     <img src={p.avatarUrl} alt="" className="h-full w-full object-cover" />
  179:                   ) : (
  180:                     (p.fullName || p.username || '?').charAt(0).toUpperCase()
  181:                   )}
  182:                 </div>
  183: 
  184:                 <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
  185:                   <div className="flex min-w-0 items-center gap-1">
  186:                     <span className="truncate text-[12.5px] font-semibold leading-[17px] text-slate-800">
  187:                       {p.fullName || p.username || `User #${p.accountId}`}
  188:                     </span>
  189:                     {p.roleInConversation === 'OWNER' && (
  190:                       <FaCrown size={10} className="shrink-0 text-amber-500" />
  191:                     )}
  192:                     {isSelf && (
  193:                       <span className="shrink-0 text-[10px] font-medium text-slate-400">
  194:                         (Bạn)
  195:                       </span>
  196:                     )}
  197:                   </div>
  198:                   <div className="mt-0.5 flex min-w-0 items-center gap-1">
  199:                     <span className={`${tag} shrink-0 ${roleTagClass[p.roleInConversation]}`}>
  200:                       {roleLabel[p.roleInConversation] || p.roleInConversation}
  201:                     </span>
  202:                     {p.chatStatus === 'MUTED' && (
  203:                       <span className={`${tag} shrink-0 bg-orange-100 text-orange-700`}>
  204:                         Mute · {formatMutedUntil(p.mutedUntil)}
  205:                       </span>
  206:                     )}
  207:                     {p.chatStatus === 'BANNED' && (
  208:                       <span className={`${tag} shrink-0 bg-rose-100 text-rose-700`}>Banned</span>
  209:                     )}
  210:                     {p.chatStatus === 'ACTIVE' && (
  211:                       <span className="truncate text-[10.5px] text-slate-400">
  212:                         {p.username || ''}
  213:                       </span>
  214:                     )}
  215:                   </div>
  216:                 </div>
  217: 
  218:                 <button
  219:                   type="button"
  220:                   onClick={(e) => openMenu(e, p.accountId)}
  221:                   disabled={isSelf}
  222:                   className={`inline-flex h-[26px] w-[26px] shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-[7px] border bg-transparent transition-colors ${
  223:                     isSelf
  224:                       ? 'cursor-default border-transparent text-transparent'
  225:                       : menu?.id === p.accountId
  226:                         ? 'border-slate-200 bg-white text-slate-700'
  227:                         : 'border-transparent text-slate-400 hover:border-slate-200 hover:bg-white hover:text-slate-700'
  228:                   }`}
  229:                   title={isSelf ? undefined : 'Tuỳ chọn thành viên'}
  230:                 >
  231:                   <FaEllipsisV size={11} />
  232:                 </button>
  233:               </div>
  234:             );
  235:           })
  236:         )}
  237:       </div>
  238: 
  239:       {menu && target && (
  240:         <>
  241:           <div className="fixed inset-0 z-[1250]" onClick={close} />
  242:           <div
  243:             className="fixed z-[1300] max-h-[300px] min-w-[196px] overflow-y-auto rounded-xl border border-slate-100 bg-white p-1 shadow-[0_12px_28px_-10px_rgba(15,23,42,0.26),0_2px_6px_rgba(15,23,42,0.06)]"
  244:             style={{ top: menu.top, left: menu.left }}
  245:           >
  246:             {isGroup && (
  247:               <button
  248:                 type="button"
  249:                 onClick={() => {
  250:                   close();
  251:                   onStartPrivateChat(target.accountId);
  252:                 }}
  253:                 className={`${menuItem} text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-50`}
  254:               >
  255:                 <FaComments size={10} className="text-brand-700" /> Nhắn tin riêng
  256:               </button>
  257:             )}
  258: 
  259:             {canManage && actionable && target.roleInConversation !== 'ADMIN' && (
  260:               <button
  261:                 type="button"
  262:                 onClick={() => {
  263:                   close();
  264:                   onGrantRole(target.accountId, 'ADMIN');
  265:                 }}
  266:                 className={`${menuItem} text-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-50`}
  267:               >
  268:                 <FaUserShield size={10} /> Cấp quyền ADMIN
  269:               </button>
  270:             )}
  271:             {canManage && actionable && target.roleInConversation === 'MEMBER' && (
  272:               <button
  273:                 type="button"
  274:                 onClick={() => {
  275:                   close();
  276:                   onGrantRole(target.accountId, 'MODERATOR');
  277:                 }}
  278:                 className={`${menuItem} text-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-50`}
  279:               >
  280:                 <FaUserShield size={10} /> Cấp quyền MODERATOR
  281:               </button>
  282:             )}
  283:             {canManage &&
  284:               actionable &&
  285:               (target.roleInConversation === 'ADMIN' ||
  286:                 target.roleInConversation === 'MODERATOR') && (
  287:                 <button
  288:                   type="button"
  289:                   onClick={() => {
  290:                     close();
  291:                     onRevokeRole(target.accountId);
  292:                   }}
  293:                   className={`${menuItem} text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-100`}
  294:                 >
  295:                   <FaUserMinus size={10} /> Thu hồi quyền
  296:                 </button>
  297:               )}
  298: 
  299:             {canModerate && actionable && target.chatStatus !== 'MUTED' && (
  300:               <>
  301:                 <div className="my-1 h-px bg-slate-100" />
  302:                 <button
  303:                   type="button"
  304:                   onClick={() => {
  305:                     close();
  306:                     onMute(target.accountId, '1h');
  307:                   }}
  308:                   className={`${menuItem} text-orange-600 hover:bg-orange-50`}
  309:                 >
  310:                   <FaMicrophoneSlash size={10} /> Mute 1 giờ
  311:                 </button>
  312:                 <button
  313:                   type="button"
  314:                   onClick={() => {
  315:                     close();
  316:                     onMute(target.accountId, '24h');
  317:                   }}
  318:                   className={`${menuItem} text-orange-600 hover:bg-orange-50`}
  319:                 >
  320:                   <FaMicrophoneSlash size={10} /> Mute 24 giờ
  321:                 </button>
  322:                 {canManage && (
  323:                   <button
  324:                     type="button"
  325:                     onClick={() => {
  326:                       close();
  327:                       onMute(target.accountId, 'forever');
  328:                     }}
  329:                     className={`${menuItem} text-orange-600 hover:bg-orange-50`}
  330:                   >
  331:                     <FaMicrophoneSlash size={10} /> Mute vĩnh viễn
  332:                   </button>
  333:                 )}
  334:               </>
  335:             )}
  336:             {canModerate && actionable && target.chatStatus === 'MUTED' && (
  337:               <button
  338:                 type="button"
  339:                 onClick={() => {
  340:                   close();
  341:                   onUnmute(target.accountId);
  342:                 }}
  343:                 className={`${menuItem} text-orange-600 hover:bg-orange-50`}
  344:               >
  345:                 <FaCheck size={10} /> Bỏ Mute
  346:               </button>
  347:             )}
  348: 
  349:             {canManage && actionable && target.chatStatus !== 'BANNED' && (
  350:               <button
  351:                 type="button"
  352:                 onClick={() => {
  353:                   close();
  354:                   onBan(target.accountId);
  355:                 }}
  356:                 className={`${menuItem} text-rose-600 hover:bg-rose-50`}
  357:               >
  358:                 <FaBan size={10} /> Cấm chat (Ban)
  359:               </button>
  360:             )}
  361:             {canManage && actionable && target.chatStatus === 'BANNED' && (
  362:               <button
  363:                 type="button"
  364:                 onClick={() => {
  365:                   close();
  366:                   onUnban(target.accountId);
  367:                 }}
  368:                 className={`${menuItem} text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-100`}
  369:               >
  370:                 <FaCheck size={10} /> Bỏ Ban
  371:               </button>
  372:             )}
  373: 
  374:             {isOwner && actionable && target.roleInConversation !== 'OWNER' && (
  375:               <button
  376:                 type="button"
  377:                 onClick={() => {
  378:                   close();
  379:                   onTransferOwner(target.accountId);
  380:                 }}
  381:                 className={`${menuItem} text-amber-600 hover:bg-amber-50`}
  382:               >
  383:                 <FaArrowUp size={10} /> Chuyển quyền OWNER
  384:               </button>
  385:             )}
  386: 
  387:             {canManage && actionable && (
  388:               <>
  389:                 <div className="my-1 h-px bg-slate-100" />
  390:                 <button
  391:                   type="button"
  392:                   onClick={() => {
  393:                     close();
  394:                     onKick(target.accountId);
  395:                   }}
  396:                   className={`${menuItem} text-rose-600 hover:bg-rose-50`}
  397:                 >
  398:                   <FaTrash size={10} /> Xoá khỏi nhóm
  399:                 </button>
  400:               </>
  401:             )}
  402:           </div>
  403:         </>
  404:       )}
  405:     </div>
  406:   );
  407: };
  408: 
  409: export default MembersView;

// ============================================================
// FILE: ./src/components/chat/MessageBubble.tsx

    1: // src/components/chat/MessageBubble.tsx
    2: import { useState } from 'react';
    3: import {
    4:   FaCheck,
    5:   FaCheckDouble,
    6:   FaDownload,
    7:   FaEdit,
    8:   FaFile,
    9:   FaReply,
   10:   FaTrash,
   11: } from 'react-icons/fa';
   12: import type { MessageResponse } from '@/services/types/chat';
   13: import { formatClock } from './chatHelpers';
   14: 
   15: interface Props {
   16:   message: MessageResponse;
   17:   isMine: boolean;
   18:   showSender?: boolean;
   19:   /** Cho phép sửa (chỉ tin của mình, chưa bị xoá, còn quyền gửi) */
   20:   canEdit?: boolean;
   21:   /** Cho phép xoá (tin của mình, hoặc MODERATOR+ xoá tin người khác) */
   22:   canDelete?: boolean;
   23:   /** Số người khác đã đọc tới tin này */
   24:   readBy?: number;
   25:   onReply?: (message: MessageResponse) => void;
   26:   onEdit?: (id: number, content: string) => void;
   27:   onDelete?: (id: number) => void;
   28: }
   29: 
   30: const formatFileSize = (bytes?: number | null) => {
   31:   if (!bytes || bytes <= 0) return '';
   32:   if (bytes < 1024) return `${bytes} B`;
   33:   if (bytes < 1024 * 1024) return `${(bytes / 1024).toFixed(1)} KB`;
   34:   return `${(bytes / (1024 * 1024)).toFixed(1)} MB`;
   35: };
   36: 
   37: const MessageBubble = ({
   38:   message,
   39:   isMine,
   40:   showSender = true,
   41:   canEdit = false,
   42:   canDelete = false,
   43:   readBy = 0,
   44:   onReply,
   45:   onEdit,
   46:   onDelete,
   47: }: Props) => {
   48:   const [showActions, setShowActions] = useState(false);
   49:   const [isEditing, setIsEditing] = useState(false);
   50:   const [editContent, setEditContent] = useState(message.content || '');
   51: 
   52:   if (message.messageType === 'SYSTEM') {
   53:     return (
   54:       <div className="flex w-full justify-center">
   55:         <div className="rounded-full bg-slate-200/70 px-3 py-1">
   56:           <div className="text-center text-[11px] italic text-slate-500">{message.content}</div>
   57:         </div>
   58:       </div>
   59:     );
   60:   }
   61: 
   62:   const handleSaveEdit = () => {
   63:     const trimmed = editContent.trim();
   64:     if (trimmed && trimmed !== (message.content || '')) {
   65:       onEdit?.(message.id, trimmed);
   66:     }
   67:     setIsEditing(false);
   68:   };
   69: 
   70:   const bubbleTone = isMine
   71:     ? 'bg-brand text-white rounded-2xl rounded-br-[5px]'
   72:     : 'border border-slate-200/80 bg-white text-slate-700 rounded-2xl rounded-bl-[5px]';
   73: 
   74:   const renderContent = () => {
   75:     if (message.isDeleted) {
   76:       return <em className="text-[11.5px] opacity-70">Tin nhắn đã bị xoá</em>;
   77:     }
   78: 
   79:     switch (message.messageType) {
   80:       case 'IMAGE':
   81:         return (
   82:           <div className="max-w-[200px]">
   83:             {message.fileUrl && (
   84:               <img
   85:                 src={message.fileUrl}
   86:                 alt={message.fileName || 'Ảnh'}
   87:                 className="block w-full cursor-pointer rounded-[9px] hover:opacity-95"
   88:                 onClick={() => window.open(message.fileUrl!, '_blank')}
   89:               />
   90:             )}
   91:             {message.content && <div className="mt-1 text-[13px]">{message.content}</div>}
   92:           </div>
   93:         );
   94:       case 'FILE':
   95:         return (
   96:           <a
   97:             href={message.fileUrl || '#'}
   98:             target="_blank"
   99:             rel="noopener noreferrer"
  100:             className={`flex min-w-[168px] items-center gap-2 rounded-[9px] p-1.5 ${
  101:               isMine ? 'bg-white/20' : 'bg-slate-50'
  102:             }`}
  103:           >
  104:             <span
  105:               className={`flex h-8 w-8 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-[8px] ${
  106:                 isMine ? 'bg-white/25 text-white' : 'bg-white text-brand-700'
  107:               }`}
  108:             >
  109:               <FaFile size={13} />
  110:             </span>
  111:             <span className="min-w-0 flex-1">
  112:               <span className="block truncate text-[11.5px] font-semibold leading-4">
  113:                 {message.fileName || 'Tài liệu'}
  114:               </span>
  115:               <span className="block text-[10px] leading-[13px] opacity-75">
  116:                 {formatFileSize(message.fileSize)}
  117:               </span>
  118:             </span>
  119:             <FaDownload size={11} className="shrink-0 opacity-60" />
  120:           </a>
  121:         );
  122:       case 'VIDEO':
  123:         return (
  124:           <div className="max-w-[200px]">
  125:             <video src={message.fileUrl || undefined} controls className="w-full rounded-[9px]">
  126:               Trình duyệt không hỗ trợ video
  127:             </video>
  128:             {message.content && <div className="mt-1 text-[13px]">{message.content}</div>}
  129:           </div>
  130:         );
  131:       case 'AUDIO':
  132:         return (
  133:           <div className="min-w-[180px]">
  134:             <audio src={message.fileUrl || undefined} controls className="w-full" />
  135:             {message.content && <div className="mt-1 text-[13px]">{message.content}</div>}
  136:           </div>
  137:         );
  138:       default:
  139:         return <div className="whitespace-pre-wrap break-words">{message.content}</div>;
  140:     }
  141:   };
  142: 
  143:   const actionsVisible = showActions && !isEditing && !message.isDeleted;
  144:   const hasActions = Boolean(onReply || (canEdit && onEdit) || (canDelete && onDelete));
  145: 
  146:   return (
  147:     <div
  148:       className={`group/msg flex w-full ${isMine ? 'justify-end' : 'justify-start'}`}
  149:       onMouseEnter={() => setShowActions(true)}
  150:       onMouseLeave={() => setShowActions(false)}
  151:     >
  152:       <div className="relative min-w-0 max-w-[72%]">
  153:         {!isMine && showSender && (
  154:           <div className="mb-0.5 ml-2 flex items-center gap-1 text-[10.5px] font-semibold leading-[13px] text-slate-400">
  155:             <span className="truncate">{message.senderName || `User #${message.senderId}`}</span>
  156:             {message.isEdited && (
  157:               <span className="shrink-0 text-[9.5px] font-normal text-slate-300">(đã sửa)</span>
  158:             )}
  159:           </div>
  160:         )}
  161: 
  162:         <div className={`px-3 py-[7px] text-[13px] leading-[1.45] ${bubbleTone}`}>
  163:           {message.replyToMessageId && (
  164:             <span
  165:               className={`mb-1 block rounded-[5px] border-l-2 px-2 py-0.5 text-[11px] leading-[15px] ${
  166:                 isMine
  167:                   ? 'border-white/50 bg-white/15 text-white/85'
  168:                   : 'border-brand bg-slate-50 text-slate-500'
  169:               }`}
  170:             >
  171:               <b className="block text-[10px] font-bold">
  172:                 {message.replyToSenderName || 'Ai đó'}
  173:               </b>
  174:               <em className="block truncate not-italic">
  175:                 {message.replyToContent || '(tin nhắn không còn)'}
  176:               </em>
  177:             </span>
  178:           )}
  179: 
  180:           {isEditing ? (
  181:             <div className="min-w-[160px]">
  182:               <input
  183:                 type="text"
  184:                 value={editContent}
  185:                 onChange={(e) => setEditContent(e.target.value)}
  186:                 autoFocus
  187:                 maxLength={10000}
  188:                 className="w-full border-none bg-transparent text-[13px] text-inherit outline-none"
  189:                 onKeyDown={(e) => {
  190:                   if (e.key === 'Enter') handleSaveEdit();
  191:                   if (e.key === 'Escape') setIsEditing(false);
  192:                 }}
  193:               />
  194:               <div className="mt-1 flex justify-end gap-2.5 text-[10px] font-bold">
  195:                 <button
  196:                   type="button"
  197:                   onClick={handleSaveEdit}
  198:                   className="cursor-pointer border-0 bg-transparent opacity-85 hover:underline hover:opacity-100"
  199:                 >
  200:                   Lưu
  201:                 </button>
  202:                 <button
  203:                   type="button"
  204:                   onClick={() => setIsEditing(false)}
  205:                   className="cursor-pointer border-0 bg-transparent opacity-85 hover:underline hover:opacity-100"
  206:                 >
  207:                   Hủy
  208:                 </button>
  209:               </div>
  210:             </div>
  211:           ) : (
  212:             renderContent()
  213:           )}
  214:         </div>
  215: 
  216:         <div
  217:           className={`mt-0.5 flex items-center gap-1 text-[10px] leading-3 text-slate-400 ${
  218:             isMine ? 'justify-end' : 'justify-start'
  219:           }`}
  220:         >
  221:           <span>{formatClock(message.createdAt)}</span>
  222:           {isMine && message.isEdited && <span className="text-slate-300">(đã sửa)</span>}
  223:           {isMine &&
  224:             (readBy > 0 ? (
  225:               <span
  226:                 className="inline-flex items-center gap-0.5 font-semibold text-brand-700"
  227:                 title={`${readBy} người đã xem`}
  228:               >
  229:                 <FaCheckDouble size={9} /> Đã xem
  230:               </span>
  231:             ) : (
  232:               <FaCheck size={9} className="text-slate-300" title="Đã gửi" />
  233:             ))}
  234:         </div>
  235: 
  236:         {actionsVisible && hasActions && (
  237:           <div
  238:             className={`pointer-events-none absolute top-0 z-[5] flex gap-px rounded-[9px] border border-slate-200 bg-white p-0.5 opacity-0 shadow-[0_8px_18px_-8px_rgba(15,23,42,0.3)] transition-opacity group-hover/msg:pointer-events-auto group-hover/msg:opacity-100 ${
  239:               isMine ? '-left-[78px]' : '-right-[78px]'
  240:             }`}
  241:           >
  242:             {onReply && (
  243:               <button
  244:                 type="button"
  245:                 onClick={() => onReply(message)}
  246:                 className="inline-flex h-[22px] w-[22px] items-center justify-center rounded-md border-0 bg-transparent text-slate-400 transition-colors hover:bg-slate-50 hover:text-brand-700"
  247:                 title="Trả lời"
  248:               >
  249:                 <FaReply size={10} />
  250:               </button>
  251:             )}
  252:             {canEdit && onEdit && (
  253:               <button
  254:                 type="button"
  255:                 onClick={() => {
  256:                   setEditContent(message.content || '');
  257:                   setIsEditing(true);
  258:                 }}
  259:                 className="inline-flex h-[22px] w-[22px] items-center justify-center rounded-md border-0 bg-transparent text-slate-400 transition-colors hover:bg-slate-50 hover:text-blue-600"
  260:                 title="Sửa"
  261:               >
  262:                 <FaEdit size={10} />
  263:               </button>
  264:             )}
  265:             {canDelete && onDelete && (
  266:               <button
  267:                 type="button"
  268:                 onClick={() => onDelete(message.id)}
  269:                 className="inline-flex h-[22px] w-[22px] items-center justify-center rounded-md border-0 bg-transparent text-slate-400 transition-colors hover:bg-rose-50 hover:text-rose-500"
  270:                 title="Xoá"
  271:               >
  272:                 <FaTrash size={10} />
  273:               </button>
  274:             )}
  275:           </div>
  276:         )}
  277:       </div>
  278:     </div>
  279:   );

// ============================================================
// FILE: ./src/components/chat/NewChatView.tsx
// ============================================================
    1: // src/components/chat/NewChatView.tsx
    2: import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
    3: import { FaComments, FaSearch, FaSpinner, FaUserPlus, FaUsers } from 'react-icons/fa';
    4: import { chatApi } from '@/services/api/chat';
    5: import type { UserSearchResponse } from '@/services/types/chat';
    6: 
    7: interface Props {
    8:   onStartChat: (user: UserSearchResponse) => void;
    9:   onCreateGroup: () => void;
   10:   /** accountId đang được mở hội thoại */
   11:   startingId?: number | null;
   12: }
   13: 
   14: interface SearchState {
   15:   keyword: string;
   16:   loading: boolean;
   17:   users: UserSearchResponse[];
   18: }
   19: 
   20: /**
   21:  * Tab "Mới": tìm người dùng để nhắn tin riêng hoặc tạo nhóm chat.
   22:  * BE: GET /api/chat/users/search + POST /api/chat/conversations (PRIVATE)
   23:  */
   24: const NewChatView = ({ onStartChat, onCreateGroup, startingId = null }: Props) => {
   25:   const [keyword, setKeyword] = useState('');
   26:   const [search, setSearch] = useState<SearchState>({
   27:     keyword: '',
   28:     loading: false,
   29:     users: [],
   30:   });
   31: 
   32:   useEffect(() => {
   33:     const kw = keyword.trim();
   34:     if (kw.length < 2) return;
   35: 
   36:     let cancelled = false;
   37:     const timer = setTimeout(async () => {
   38:       if (cancelled) return;
   39:       setSearch((prev) => ({ ...prev, keyword: kw, loading: true }));
   40:       try {
   41:         const res = await chatApi.searchUsers(kw);
   42:         if (!cancelled) {
   43:           setSearch({ keyword: kw, loading: false, users: Array.isArray(res) ? res : [] });
   44:         }
   45:       } catch {
   46:         if (!cancelled) setSearch({ keyword: kw, loading: false, users: [] });
   47:       }
   48:     }, 350);
   49: 
   50:     return () => {
   51:       cancelled = true;
   52:       clearTimeout(timer);
   53:     };
   54:   }, [keyword]);
   55: 
   56:   const kw = keyword.trim();
   57:   const isActive = kw.length >= 2;
   58:   const isFresh = isActive && search.keyword === kw;
   59:   const loading = isActive && (search.loading || !isFresh);
   60:   const users = isFresh ? search.users : [];
   61: 
   62:   return (
   63:     <div className="flex min-h-0 flex-1 flex-col bg-white">
   64:       <div className="flex shrink-0 flex-col gap-2 border-b border-slate-100 px-3 py-2.5">
   65:         <button
   66:           type="button"
   67:           onClick={onCreateGroup}
   68:           className="flex h-9 w-full items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl bg-brand text-xs font-bold text-white transition-colors hover:bg-brand-600"
   69:         >
   70:           <FaUsers size={12} />
   71:           Tạo nhóm chat mới
   72:         </button>
   73:         <div className="relative">
   74:           <FaSearch
   75:             size={11}
   76:             className="pointer-events-none absolute left-2.5 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-slate-400"
   77:           />
   78:           <input
   79:             type="text"
   80:             value={keyword}
   81:             onChange={(e) => setKeyword(e.target.value)}
   82:             placeholder="Tìm người dùng (tên, email...)"
   83:             autoFocus
   84:             className="h-9 w-full rounded-[10px] border border-slate-200 bg-slate-50 pl-8 pr-2.5 text-xs text-slate-700 outline-none transition placeholder:text-slate-400 focus:border-brand focus:bg-white focus:ring-[3px] focus:ring-brand/15"
   85:           />
   86:         </div>
   87:       </div>
   88: 
   89:       <div className="flex min-h-0 flex-1 flex-col gap-1.5 overflow-y-auto p-3">
   90:         {!isActive ? (
   91:           <div className="flex h-full flex-col items-center justify-center gap-1 text-center">
   92:             <FaUserPlus className="mb-1 text-[26px] text-slate-300" />
   93:             <div className="text-[12.5px] font-semibold text-slate-500">Tìm kiếm người dùng</div>
   94:             <div className="text-[11px] text-slate-400">Nhập ít nhất 2 ký tự</div>
   95:           </div>
   96:         ) : loading ? (
   97:           <div className="flex h-full items-center justify-center text-brand">
   98:             <FaSpinner className="animate-spin" size={22} />
   99:           </div>
  100:         ) : users.length === 0 ? (
  101:           <div className="flex h-full flex-col items-center justify-center gap-1 text-center">
  102:             <FaSearch className="mb-1 text-[26px] text-slate-300" />
  103:             <div className="text-[12.5px] font-semibold text-slate-500">
  104:               Không tìm thấy người dùng
  105:             </div>
  106:             <div className="text-[11px] text-slate-400">Thử từ khoá khác</div>
  107:           </div>
  108:         ) : (
  109:           users.map((u) => (
  110:             <div
  111:               key={u.accountId}
  112:               className="flex items-center gap-2.5 rounded-[10px] border border-slate-100 p-2 transition-colors hover:bg-slate-50"
  113:             >
  114:               <div className="flex h-8 w-8 shrink-0 items-center justify-center overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-brand/10 text-xs font-bold text-brand">
  115:                 {u.avatarUrl ? (
  116:                   <img src={u.avatarUrl} alt="" className="h-full w-full object-cover" />
  117:                 ) : (
  118:                   (u.fullName || u.username || '?').charAt(0).toUpperCase()
  119:                 )}
  120:               </div>
  121:               <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
  122:                 <div className="truncate text-xs font-semibold leading-4 text-slate-800">
  123:                   {u.fullName || u.username || `User #${u.accountId}`}
  124:                 </div>
  125:                 <div className="truncate text-[10.5px] leading-[14px] text-slate-400">
  126:                   {u.email || u.phone || u.username}
  127:                 </div>
  128:               </div>
  129:               <button
  130:                 type="button"
  131:                 onClick={() => onStartChat(u)}
  132:                 disabled={startingId === u.accountId}
  133:                 className="inline-flex h-[26px] shrink-0 items-center gap-1 rounded-full border border-brand bg-white px-2 text-[10.5px] font-bold text-brand-700 transition-colors hover:bg-brand hover:text-white disabled:opacity-60"
  134:               >
  135:                 {startingId === u.accountId ? (
  136:                   <FaSpinner className="animate-spin" size={10} />
  137:                 ) : (
  138:                   <>
  139:                     <FaComments size={10} />
  140:                     {u.hasPrivateConversation ? 'Vào chat' : 'Nhắn tin'}
  141:                   </>
  142:                 )}
  143:               </button>
  144:             </div>
  145:           ))
  146:         )}
  147:       </div>
  148:     </div>
  149:   );
  150: };
  151: 
  152: export default NewChatView;

    1: // src/components/chat/UserPicker.tsx
    2: import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react';
    3: import { FaCheck, FaSearch, FaSpinner, FaUserPlus } from 'react-icons/fa';
    4: import { chatApi } from '@/services/api/chat';
    5: import type { UserSearchResponse } from '@/services/types/chat';
    6: 
    7: interface Props {
    8:   /** accountId đang được chọn */
    9:   selectedIds: number[];
   10:   onToggle: (user: UserSearchResponse) => void;
   11:   /** accountId bị loại khỏi danh sách (đã ở trong nhóm) */
   12:   excludeIds?: number[];
   13:   /** Số ký tự tối thiểu trước khi gọi API (BE yêu cầu >= 2) */
   14:   minChars?: number;
   15:   autoFocus?: boolean;
   16:   placeholder?: string;
   17:   emptyHint?: string;
   18:   /** Nội dung gợi ý khi chưa nhập từ khoá */
   19:   idleHint?: string;
   20: }
   21: 
   22: interface SearchState {
   23:   keyword: string;
   24:   loading: boolean;
   25:   users: UserSearchResponse[];
   26: }
   27: 
   28: /**
   29:  * Ô tìm kiếm + danh sách người dùng để chọn (dùng cho Tạo nhóm / Thêm thành viên).
   30:  * BE: GET /api/chat/users/search?keyword=
   31:  */
   32: const UserPicker = ({
   33:   selectedIds,
   34:   onToggle,
   35:   excludeIds = [],
   36:   minChars = 2,
   37:   autoFocus = false,
   38:   placeholder = 'Tìm người dùng...',
   39:   emptyHint = 'Không tìm thấy người dùng',
   40:   idleHint = 'Nhập ít nhất 2 ký tự để tìm kiếm',
   41: }: Props) => {
   42:   const [keyword, setKeyword] = useState('');
   43:   const [search, setSearch] = useState<SearchState>({
   44:     keyword: '',
   45:     loading: false,
   46:     users: [],
   47:   });
   48: 
   49:   const excluded = useMemo(() => new Set(excludeIds), [excludeIds]);
   50: 
   51:   useEffect(() => {
   52:     const kw = keyword.trim();
   53:     if (kw.length < minChars) return;
   54: 
   55:     let cancelled = false;
   56:     const timer = setTimeout(async () => {
   57:       if (cancelled) return;
   58:       setSearch((prev) => ({ ...prev, keyword: kw, loading: true }));
   59:       try {
   60:         const res = await chatApi.searchUsers(kw);
   61:         if (!cancelled) {
   62:           setSearch({ keyword: kw, loading: false, users: Array.isArray(res) ? res : [] });
   63:         }
   64:       } catch {
   65:         if (!cancelled) setSearch({ keyword: kw, loading: false, users: [] });
   66:       }
   67:     }, 350);
   68: 
   69:     return () => {
   70:       cancelled = true;
   71:       clearTimeout(timer);
   72:     };
   73:   }, [keyword, minChars]);
   74: 
   75:   const kw = keyword.trim();
   76:   const isActive = kw.length >= minChars;
   77:   const isFresh = isActive && search.keyword === kw;
   78:   const loading = isActive && (search.loading || !isFresh);
   79:   const users = isFresh ? search.users : [];
   80:   const visible = users.filter((u) => !excluded.has(u.accountId));
   81: 
   82:   return (
   83:     <div className="flex min-h-0 flex-1 flex-col">
   84:       <div className="relative shrink-0 px-3 py-2">
   85:         <FaSearch
   86:           size={11}
   87:           className="pointer-events-none absolute left-[22px] top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-slate-400"
   88:         />
   89:         <input
   90:           type="text"
   91:           value={keyword}
   92:           onChange={(e) => setKeyword(e.target.value)}
   93:           placeholder={placeholder}
   94:           autoFocus={autoFocus}
   95:           className="h-9 w-full rounded-[10px] border border-slate-200 bg-slate-50 pl-8 pr-2.5 text-xs text-slate-700 outline-none transition placeholder:text-slate-400 focus:border-brand focus:bg-white focus:ring-[3px] focus:ring-brand/15"
   96:         />
   97:       </div>
   98: 
   99:       <div className="flex min-h-0 flex-1 flex-col gap-1 overflow-y-auto px-3 pb-3">
  100:         {!isActive ? (
  101:           <div className="flex h-full flex-col items-center justify-center gap-1 text-center">
  102:             <FaUserPlus className="mb-1 text-[26px] text-slate-300" />
  103:             <div className="text-[12.5px] font-semibold text-slate-500">Tìm người dùng</div>
  104:             <div className="text-[11px] text-slate-400">{idleHint}</div>
  105:           </div>
  106:         ) : loading ? (
  107:           <div className="flex h-full items-center justify-center text-brand">
  108:             <FaSpinner className="animate-spin" size={20} />
  109:           </div>
  110:         ) : visible.length === 0 ? (
  111:           <div className="flex h-full flex-col items-center justify-center gap-1 text-center">
  112:             <FaSearch className="mb-1 text-[26px] text-slate-300" />
  113:             <div className="text-[12.5px] font-semibold text-slate-500">{emptyHint}</div>
  114:           </div>
  115:         ) : (
  116:           visible.map((u) => {
  117:             const active = selectedIds.includes(u.accountId);
  118:             return (
  119:               <button
  120:                 type="button"
  121:                 key={u.accountId}
  122:                 onClick={() => onToggle(u)}
  123:                 className={`flex w-full items-center gap-2.5 rounded-[10px] border bg-white p-2 text-left transition-colors ${
  124:                   active
  125:                     ? 'border-brand bg-brand/[0.06]'
  126:                     : 'border-slate-100 hover:bg-slate-50'
  127:                 }`}
  128:               >
  129:                 <div className="flex h-8 w-8 shrink-0 items-center justify-center overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-brand/10 text-xs font-bold text-brand">
  130:                   {u.avatarUrl ? (
  131:                     <img src={u.avatarUrl} alt="" className="h-full w-full object-cover" />
  132:                   ) : (
  133:                     (u.fullName || u.username || '?').charAt(0).toUpperCase()
  134:                   )}
  135:                 </div>
  136:                 <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
  137:                   <div className="truncate text-xs font-semibold leading-4 text-slate-800">
  138:                     {u.fullName || u.username || `User #${u.accountId}`}
  139:                   </div>

// ============================================================
// FILE: ./src/components/common/ConfirmDelete.tsx
// ============================================================
    1: import { Popconfirm } from 'antd';
    2: import { DeleteOutlined } from '@ant-design/icons';
    3: import type { ReactNode } from 'react';
    4: import { useState } from 'react';
    5: import toast from 'react-hot-toast';
    6: 
    7: interface Props {
    8:   title?: string;
    9:   description?: string;
   10:   onConfirm: () => Promise<void>;
   11:   onSuccess?: () => void;
   12:   children?: ReactNode;
   13: }
   14: 
   15: const ConfirmDelete = ({
   16:   title = 'Xác nhận xóa',
   17:   description = 'Bạn có chắc chắn muốn xóa? Hành động này không thể hoàn tác.',
   18:   onConfirm,
   19:   onSuccess,
   20:   children,
   21: }: Props) => {
   22:   const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
   23: 
   24:   const handleConfirm = async () => {
   25:     setLoading(true);
   26:     try {
   27:       await onConfirm();
   28:       toast.success('Xóa thành công');
   29:       onSuccess?.();
   30:     } catch (error) {
   31:       const err = error as { message?: string };
   32:       toast.error(err?.message || 'Xóa thất bại');
   33:     } finally {
   34:       setLoading(false);
   35:     }
   36:   };
   37: 
   38:   return (
   39:     <Popconfirm
   40:       title={title}
   41:       description={description}
   42:       onConfirm={handleConfirm}
   43:       okText="Xóa"
   44:       cancelText="Hủy"
   45:       okButtonProps={{ danger: true, loading }}
   46:     >
   47:       {children || <DeleteOutlined style={{ color: '#ff4d4f', cursor: 'pointer' }} />}
   48:     </Popconfirm>
   49:   );
   50: };
   51: 
   52: export default ConfirmDelete;

// ============================================================
// FILE: ./src/components/common/DataTable.tsx
// ============================================================
    1: import { Table, Space } from 'antd';
    2: import type { ColumnsType, TablePaginationConfig } from 'antd/es/table';
    3: import type { ReactNode, HTMLAttributes } from 'react';
    4: import SearchInput from './SearchInput';
    5: import EmptyState from '../feedback/EmptyState';
    6: import { useTranslation } from '@/hooks/useTranslation';
    7: 
    8: interface Props<T> {
    9:   columns: ColumnsType<T>;
   10:   dataSource: T[];
   11:   loading?: boolean;
   12:   rowKey?: string | ((record: T) => string | number);
   13: 
   14:   // Pagination
   15:   total?: number;
   16:   page?: number;
   17:   pageSize?: number;
   18:   onPageChange?: (page: number, pageSize: number) => void;
   19: 
   20:   // Toolbar
   21:   onSearch?: (value: string) => void;
   22:   searchPlaceholder?: string;
   23:   extra?: ReactNode;
   24:   title?: ReactNode;
   25:   showSearch?: boolean;
   26:   
   27:   // Event
   28:   onRow?: (record: T, index?: number) => HTMLAttributes<HTMLElement>;
   29: }
   30: 
   31: function DataTable<T extends object>({
   32:   columns,
   33:   dataSource,
   34:   loading = false,
   35:   rowKey = 'id',
   36:   total,
   37:   page = 0,
   38:   pageSize = 20,
   39:   onPageChange,
   40:   onSearch,
   41:   searchPlaceholder,
   42:   extra,
   43:   title,
   44:   showSearch = true,
   45:   onRow,
   46: }: Props<T>) {
   47:   const { t } = useTranslation();
   48: 
   49:   const pagination: TablePaginationConfig | false =
   50:     total !== undefined
   51:       ? {
   52:           current: page + 1,
   53:           pageSize,
   54:           total,
   55:           showSizeChanger: true,
   56:           showTotal: (totalCount, range) => `${range[0]}-${range[1]} ${t('common.paginationOf')} ${totalCount}`,
   57:           onChange: (p, ps) => onPageChange?.(p - 1, ps),
   58:           pageSizeOptions: ['10', '20', '50', '100'],
   59:         }
   60:       : false;
   61: 
   62:   return (
   63:     <div>
   64:       <div
   65:         style={{
   66:           display: 'flex',
   67:           justifyContent: 'space-between',
   68:           alignItems: 'center',
   69:           marginBottom: 16,
   70:           gap: 12,
   71:           flexWrap: 'wrap',
   72:         }}
   73:       >
   74:         <Space>
   75:           {showSearch && onSearch && (
   76:             <SearchInput onSearch={onSearch} placeholder={searchPlaceholder} />
   77:           )}
   78:         </Space>
   79:         <Space>{extra}</Space>
   80:       </div>
   81: 
   82:       <Table<T>
   83:         className="modern-table"
   84:         columns={columns}
   85:         dataSource={dataSource}
   86:         loading={loading}
   87:         rowKey={rowKey}
   88:         pagination={pagination}
   89:         title={title ? () => title : undefined}
   90:         scroll={{ x: 'max-content' }}
   91:         onRow={onRow}
   92:         locale={{
   93:           emptyText: <EmptyState />,
   94:         }}
   95:       />
   96:     </div>
   97:   );
   98: }
   99: 
  100: export default DataTable;

// ============================================================
// FILE: ./src/components/common/DateDisplay.tsx
// ============================================================
    1: import { formatDate, formatDateTime, formatRelative } from '@/utils/format';
    2: 
    3: interface Props {
    4:   value?: string | Date | null;
    5:   format?: 'short' | 'long' | 'relative';
    6: }
    7: 
    8: const DateDisplay = ({ value, format = 'short' }: Props) => {
    9:   if (!value) return <span>—</span>;
   10: 
   11:   const text =
   12:     format === 'long' ? formatDateTime(value) : format === 'relative' ? formatRelative(value) : formatDate(value);
   13: 
   14:   return <span>{text}</span>;
   15: };
   16: 
   17: export default DateDisplay;

// FILE: ./src/components/common/InlineEdit.tsx
// ============================================================
    1: import { useState, useRef, useEffect } from 'react';
    2: import { Input, Popconfirm, InputNumber, Select, Switch } from 'antd';
    3: 
    4: 
    5: type EditType = 'text' | 'textarea' | 'number' | 'select' | 'switch';
    6: 
    7: interface Props {
    8:   value: any;
    9:   onSave: (newValue: any) => Promise<void> | void;
   10:   type?: EditType;
   11:   options?: { label: string; value: any }[];
   12:   formatDisplay?: (val: any) => React.ReactNode;
   13:   placeholder?: string;
   14: }
   15: 
   16: const InlineEdit = ({
   17:   value,
   18:   onSave,
   19:   type = 'text',
   20:   options = [],
   21:   formatDisplay,
   22:   placeholder = 'Nhập...',
   23: }: Props) => {
   24:   const [isEditing, setIsEditing] = useState(false);
   25:   const [showConfirm, setShowConfirm] = useState(false);
   26:   const [tempValue, setTempValue] = useState<any>(value);
   27:   const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
   28:   const inputRef = useRef<any>(null);
   29:   const wrapperRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
   30: 
   31:   // Sync tempValue khi value thay đổi từ bên ngoài (sau khi save/refresh)
   32:   useEffect(() => {
   33:     if (!isEditing) {
   34:       setTempValue(value);
   35:     }
   36:   }, [value, isEditing]);
   37: 
   38:   // Hủy edit khi click ra ngoài wrapper
   39:   useEffect(() => {
   40:     if (!isEditing) return;
   41: 
   42:     const handleClickOutside = (event: MouseEvent) => {
   43:       const target = event.target as HTMLElement;
   44: 
   45:       // Bỏ qua nếu click vào popup Ant Design (Popconfirm, Select dropdown, Modal...)
   46:       if (
   47:         target.closest(
   48:           '.ant-popover, .ant-select-dropdown, .ant-modal, .ant-picker-dropdown, .ant-notification, .ant-message'
   49:         )
   50:       ) {
   51:         return;
   52:       }
   53: 
   54:       // Bỏ qua nếu click trong wrapper của component này
   55:       if (wrapperRef.current && wrapperRef.current.contains(target)) {
   56:         return;
   57:       }
   58: 
   59:       // Click ra ngoài → hủy
   60:       setIsEditing(false);
   61:       setShowConfirm(false);
   62:       setTempValue(value);
   63:     };
   64: 
   65:     // Delay gắn listener để không bắt nhầm sự kiện double-click đang trigger
   66:     const timer = setTimeout(() => {
   67:       document.addEventListener('mousedown', handleClickOutside);
   68:     }, 100);
   69: 
   70:     return () => {
   71:       clearTimeout(timer);
   72:       document.removeEventListener('mousedown', handleClickOutside);
   73:     };
   74:   }, [isEditing, value]);
   75: 
   76:   const handleDoubleClick = () => {
   77:     setTempValue(value);
   78:     setIsEditing(true);
   79:     setTimeout(() => {
   80:       if (type === 'text' || type === 'textarea') inputRef.current?.focus();
   81:     }, 50);
   82:   };
   83: 
   84:   const cancelEdit = () => {
   85:     setIsEditing(false);
   86:     setShowConfirm(false);
   87:     setTempValue(value);
   88:   };
   89: 
   90:   const handleConfirm = async () => {
   91:     // Không thay đổi hoặc rỗng với trường text → coi như cancel
   92:     if (
   93:       tempValue === value ||
   94:       (type !== 'switch' && type !== 'number' && !tempValue?.toString().trim())
   95:     ) {
   96:       cancelEdit();
   97:       return;
   98:     }
   99:     setSaving(true);
  100:     try {
  101:       await onSave(tempValue);
  102:       setIsEditing(false);
  103:       setShowConfirm(false);
  104:     } catch (error) {
  105:       // Giữ nguyên trạng thái editing để user sửa lại
  106:     } finally {
  107:       setSaving(false);
  108:     }
  109:   };
  110: 
  111:   const handleKeyDown = (e: React.KeyboardEvent) => {
  112:     if (e.key === 'Enter' && !e.shiftKey && type !== 'textarea') {
  113:       e.preventDefault();
  114:       setShowConfirm(true);
  115:     } else if (e.key === 'Escape') {
  116:       cancelEdit();
  117:     }
  118:   };
  119: 
  120:   const renderInput = () => {
  121:     switch (type) {
  122:       case 'textarea':
  123:         return (
  124:           <Input.TextArea
  125:             ref={inputRef}
  126:             value={tempValue}
  127:             onChange={(e) => setTempValue(e.target.value)}
  128:             onKeyDown={handleKeyDown}
  129:             autoSize={{ minRows: 2, maxRows: 5 }}
  130:             placeholder={placeholder}
  131:             disabled={saving}
  132:           />
  133:         );
  134:       case 'number':
  135:         return (
  136:           <InputNumber
  137:             ref={inputRef}
  138:             value={tempValue}
  139:             onChange={(val) => setTempValue(val)}
  140:             onKeyDown={handleKeyDown}
  141:             style={{ width: '100%' }}
  142:             placeholder={placeholder}
  143:             disabled={saving}
  144:             formatter={(v) => (v ? `${v}`.replace(/\B(?=(\d{3})+(?!\d))/g, ',') : '')}
  145:             parser={(v) => v!.replace(/\$\s?|(,*)/g, '')}
  146:           />
  147:         );
  148:       case 'select':
  149:         return (
  150:           <Select
  151:             ref={inputRef}
  152:             value={tempValue}
  153:             onChange={(val) => {
  154:               setTempValue(val);
  155:               setShowConfirm(true);
  156:             }}
  157:             options={options}
  158:             style={{ width: '100%', minWidth: 200 }}
  159:             placeholder={placeholder}
  160:             disabled={saving}
  161:             autoFocus
  162:           />
  163:         );
  164:       case 'switch':
  165:         return (
  166:           <Switch
  167:             checked={tempValue === true}
  168:             onChange={(val) => {
  169:               setTempValue(val);
  170:               setShowConfirm(true);
  171:             }}
  172:             disabled={saving}
  173:           />
  174:         );
  175:       default:
  176:         return (
  177:           <Input
  178:             ref={inputRef}
  179:             value={tempValue}
  180:             onChange={(e) => setTempValue(e.target.value)}
  181:             onKeyDown={handleKeyDown}
  182:             placeholder={placeholder}
  183:             disabled={saving}
  184:           />
  185:         );
  186:     }
  187:   };
  188: 
  189:   if (isEditing) {
  190:     return (
  191:       <div ref={wrapperRef}>
  192:         <Popconfirm
  193:           title="Xác nhận sửa?"
  194:           description="Bạn có chắc chắn muốn lưu thay đổi này?"
  195:           open={showConfirm}
  196:           onConfirm={handleConfirm}
  197:           onCancel={cancelEdit}
  198:           okText="Xác nhận"
  199:           cancelText="Hủy"
  200:           okButtonProps={{ loading: saving }}
  201:         >
  202:           <div
  203:             style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 4 }}
  204:             onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
  205:           >
  206:             {renderInput()}
  207:             {type !== 'select' && type !== 'switch' && (
  208:               <div style={{ fontSize: 11, color: '#888' }}>
  209:                 {type === 'textarea'
  210:                   ? 'Esc hoặc click ra ngoài để hủy'
  211:                   : 'Enter để xác nhận · Esc / click ra ngoài để hủy'}
  212:               </div>
  213:             )}
  214:           </div>
  215:         </Popconfirm>
  216:       </div>
  217:     );
  218:   }
  219: 
  220:   // Chế độ Read-only
  221:   return (
  222:     <div
  223:       onDoubleClick={handleDoubleClick}
  224:       style={{
  225:         cursor: 'pointer',
  226:         minHeight: 22,
  227:         padding: '4px 8px',
  228:         margin: '-4px -8px',
  229:         borderRadius: 4,
  230:         transition: 'background 0.2s',
  231:         display: 'inline-block',
  232:         minWidth: 100,
  233:       }}
  234:       onMouseEnter={(e) => (e.currentTarget.style.background = '#f0f0f0')}
  235:       onMouseLeave={(e) => (e.currentTarget.style.background = 'transparent')}
  236:       title="Nhấp đúp để sửa"
  237:     >
  238:       {formatDisplay
  239:         ? formatDisplay(value)
  240:         : value || <span style={{ color: '#bfbfbf' }}>(Chưa có thông tin)</span>}
  241:     </div>
  242:   );
  243: };
  244: 
  245: export default InlineEdit;

// FILE: ./src/components/common/PageHeader.tsx
// ============================================================
    1: import { Button, Space } from 'antd';
    2: import { ArrowLeftOutlined } from '@ant-design/icons';
    3: import type { ReactNode } from 'react';
    4: import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
    5: 
    6: interface Props {
    7:   title: string;
    8:   subtitle?: ReactNode;
    9:   extra?: ReactNode;
   10:   backButton?: boolean;
   11:   backTo?: string;
   12:   badge?: ReactNode;
   13: }
   14: 
   15: const PageHeader = ({ title, subtitle, extra, backButton, backTo, badge }: Props) => {
   16:   const navigate = useNavigate();
   17: 
   18:   const handleBack = () => {
   19:     if (backTo) navigate(backTo);
   20:     else navigate(-1);
   21:   };
   22: 
   23:   return (
   24:     <div
   25:       style={{
   26:         display: 'flex',
   27:         justifyContent: 'space-between',
   28:         alignItems: 'center',
   29:         marginBottom: 20,
   30:         gap: 16,
   31:         flexWrap: 'wrap',
   32:       }}
   33:     >
   34:       <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 12 }}>
   35:         {backButton && (
   36:           <Button
   37:             type="text"
   38:             icon={<ArrowLeftOutlined />}
   39:             onClick={handleBack}
   40:             style={{
   41:               borderRadius: 8,
   42:               border: '1px solid #e2e8f0',
   43:               background: '#ffffff',
   44:             }}
   45:           />
   46:         )}
   47:         <div>
   48:           <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 10 }}>
   49:             <h1
   50:               style={{
   51:                 fontSize: 22,
   52:                 fontWeight: 700,
   53:                 margin: 0,
   54:                 color: '#0f172a',
   55:                 lineHeight: 1.25,
   56:                 letterSpacing: '-0.02em',
   57:               }}
   58:             >
   59:               {title}
   60:             </h1>
   61:             {badge}
   62:           </div>
   63:           {subtitle && (
   64:             <p style={{ margin: '4px 0 0 0', color: '#64748b', fontSize: 13, lineHeight: 1.4 }}>
   65:               {subtitle}
   66:             </p>
   67:           )}
   68:         </div>
   69:       </div>
   70: 
   71:       {extra && <Space size={10}>{extra}</Space>}
   72:     </div>
   73:   );
   74: };
   75: 
   76: export default PageHeader;

// FILE: ./src/components/common/SearchInput.tsx
// ============================================================
    1: import { Input } from 'antd';
    2: import { SearchOutlined } from '@ant-design/icons';
    3: import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
    4: import { useDebounce } from '@/hooks/useDebounce';
    5: 
    6: interface Props {
    7:   onSearch: (value: string) => void;
    8:   placeholder?: string;
    9:   delay?: number;
   10:   width?: number | string;
   11: }
   12: 
   13: const SearchInput = ({
   14:   onSearch,
   15:   placeholder = 'Tìm kiếm...',
   16:   delay = 500,
   17:   width = 320,
   18: }: Props) => {
   19:   const [value, setValue] = useState('');
   20:   const debounced = useDebounce(value, delay);
   21: 
   22:   useEffect(() => {
   23:     onSearch(debounced);
   24:   }, [debounced, onSearch]);
   25: 
   26:   return (
   27:     <Input
   28:       allowClear
   29:       prefix={<SearchOutlined style={{ color: '#94a3b8', marginRight: 4 }} />}
   30:       placeholder={placeholder}
   31:       value={value}
   32:       onChange={(e) => setValue(e.target.value)}
   33:       style={{
   34:         width,
   35:         borderRadius: 8,
   36:         height: 38,
   37:         fontSize: 13,
   38:         borderColor: '#e2e8f0',
   39:         boxShadow: '0 1px 2px rgba(0,0,0,0.03)',
   40:         transition: 'all 0.2s ease',
   41:       }}
   42:     />
   43:   );
   44: };
   45: 
   46: export default SearchInput;

// FILE: ./src/components/common/StatsCard.tsx

    1: import React from 'react';
    2: import { Card, Typography, Tooltip, Tag } from 'antd';
    3: 
    4: const { Text } = Typography;
    5: 
    6: export interface StatsCardProps {
    7:   title: string;
    8:   value: number | string;
    9:   icon: React.ReactNode;
   10:   color?: string;
   11:   bgColor?: string;
   12:   active?: boolean;
   13:   onClick?: () => void;
   14:   tooltip?: string;
   15:   tagText?: string;
   16:   tagColor?: string;
   17:   suffix?: string;
   18:   trend?: {
   19:     value: number | string;
   20:     isUp?: boolean;
   21:     label?: string;
   22:   };
   23: }
   24: 
   25: const StatsCard: React.FC<StatsCardProps> = ({
   26:   title,
   27:   value,
   28:   icon,
   29:   color = '#1677ff',
   30:   bgColor = '#e6f4ff',
   31:   active = false,
   32:   onClick,
   33:   tooltip,
   34:   tagText,
   35:   tagColor = 'default',
   36:   suffix,
   37: }) => {
   38:   const content = (
   39:     <Card
   40:       className={`stat-card-modern ${active ? 'active' : ''}`}
   41:       onClick={onClick}
   42:       bordered={false}
   43:       style={{
   44:         background: '#ffffff',
   45:         height: '100%',
   46:         borderRadius: 14,
   47:         position: 'relative',
   48:         overflow: 'hidden',
   49:         border: active ? `1.5px solid ${color}` : '1px solid #e2e8f0',

   54:         cursor: onClick ? 'pointer' : 'default',

   62:             position: 'absolute',

   64:             left: 0,

   83:                 textOverflow: 'ellipsis',
   84:                 letterSpacing: '0.01em',
   85:               }}
   86:             >
   87:               {title}
   88:             </Text>
   89:             {tagText && (
   90:               <Tag
   91:                 color={tagColor}
   92:                 style={{
   93:                   margin: 0,
   94:                   fontSize: 11,
   95:                   padding: '1px 8px',
   96:                   borderRadius: 20,
   97:                   fontWeight: 600,
   98:                   border: 'none',
   99:                 }}
  100:               >
  101:                 {tagText}
  102:               </Tag>
  103:             )}
  104:           </div>
  105:

  107:             <span
  108:               style={{
  109:                 fontSize: 26,

  111:                 color: active ? color : '#0f172a',

  116:               }}
  117:             >

  119:             </span>
  120:             {suffix && (
  121:               <Text type="secondary" style={{ fontSize: 13, fontWeight: 500 }}>
  122:                 {suffix}
  123:               </Text>
  124:             )}
  125:           </div>
  126:         </div>
  127: 
  128:         <div
  129:           style={{
  130:             width: 46,
  131:             height: 46,
  132:             borderRadius: 12,
  133:             backgroundColor: bgColor,
  134:             color: color,
  135:             display: 'flex',
  136:             alignItems: 'center',
  137:             justifyContent: 'center',
  138:             fontSize: 22,
  139:             flexShrink: 0,
  140:             marginLeft: 14,
  141:             boxShadow: `0 4px 12px ${color}18`,
  142:             transition: 'transform 0.25s ease',
  143:           }}
  144:           className="stat-icon-wrapper"
  145:         >
  146:           {icon}
  147:         </div>
  148:       </div>
  149:     </Card>
  150:   );
  151: 
  152:   if (tooltip) {
  153:     return (
  154:       <Tooltip title={tooltip} mouseEnterDelay={0.4}>
  155:         {content}
  156:       </Tooltip>
  157:     );
  158:   }
  159: 
  160:   return content;
  161: };
  162: 
  163: export default StatsCard;

// ============================================================
// FILE: ./src/components/common/StatusTag.tsx
// ============================================================
    1: import { Tag } from 'antd';
    2: import { useTranslation } from '@/hooks/useTranslation';
    3: 
    4: type StatusType = 'course' | 'session' | 'question' | 'resource' | 'role' | 'custom';
    5: 
    6: interface Props {
    7:   status: string;
    8:   type?: StatusType;
    9:   showIcon?: boolean;
   10: }
   11: 
   12: const STATUS_COLOR_MAP: Record<string, string> = {
   13:   // Lead
   14:   NEW: 'blue',
   15:   CONSULTED: 'green',
   16:   // Course
   17:   DRAFT: 'default',
   18:   PENDING_REVIEW: 'orange',
   19:   PUBLISHED: 'green',
   20:   ARCHIVED: 'purple',
   21:   CANCELLED: 'red',
   22:   // Class
   23:   PLANNED: 'blue',
   24:   OPEN: 'cyan',
   25:   ONGOING: 'processing',
   26:   FINISHED: 'green',
   27:   // Session
   28:   SCHEDULED: 'blue',
   29:   DONE: 'green',
   30:   RESCHEDULED: 'orange',
   31:   // Active/Inactive
   32:   ACTIVE: 'green',
   33:   INACTIVE: 'default',
   34:   HIDDEN: 'default',
   35:   // Roles
   36:   ADMIN: 'red',
   37:   INSTRUCTOR: 'blue',
   38:   STUDENT: 'green',
   39:   // Types
   40:   ONLINE: 'cyan',
   41:   OFFLINE: 'gold',
   42: };
   43: 
   44: const StatusTag = ({ status, showIcon = false }: Props) => {
   45:   const { t } = useTranslation();
   46:   const color = STATUS_COLOR_MAP[status] || 'default';
   47:   const label = t(`status.${status}`, status);
   48: 
   49:   return (
   50:     <Tag color={color} icon={showIcon ? undefined : undefined}>
   51:       {label}
   52:     </Tag>
   53:   );
   54: };
   55: 
   56: export default StatusTag;

// ============================================================
// FILE: ./src/components/common/UserAvatar.tsx
// ============================================================
    1: import React from 'react';
    2: import { Avatar } from 'antd';
    3: import { UserOutlined } from '@ant-design/icons';
    4: import { getInitials } from '@/utils/format';
    5: 
    6: interface Props {
    7:   name?: string;
    8:   avatarUrl?: string;
    9:   size?: number | 'small' | 'default' | 'large';
   10:   showName?: boolean;
   11:   style?: React.CSSProperties;
   12: }
   13: 
   14: const AVATAR_PALETTE = [
   15:   { bg: '#dbeafe', color: '#1d4ed8', border: '#bfdbfe' }, // blue
   16:   { bg: '#ede9fe', color: '#6d28d9', border: '#ddd6fe' }, // purple
   17:   { bg: '#d1fae5', color: '#047857', border: '#a7f3d0' }, // emerald
   18:   { bg: '#fef3c7', color: '#b45309', border: '#fde68a' }, // amber
   19:   { bg: '#fce7f3', color: '#be185d', border: '#fbcfe8' }, // pink
   20:   { bg: '#ccfbf1', color: '#0f766e', border: '#99f6e4' }, // teal
   21:   { bg: '#e0e7ff', color: '#4338ca', border: '#c7d2fe' }, // indigo
   22: ];
   23: 
   24: function getAvatarPalette(name?: string) {
   25:   if (!name) return AVATAR_PALETTE[0];
   26:   let hash = 0;
   27:   for (let i = 0; i < name.length; i++) {
   28:     hash = name.charCodeAt(i) + ((hash << 5) - hash);
   29:   }
   30:   const index = Math.abs(hash) % AVATAR_PALETTE.length;
   31:   return AVATAR_PALETTE[index];
   32: }
   33: 
   34: const UserAvatar = ({
   35:   name,
   36:   avatarUrl,
   37:   size = 32,
   38:   showName = false,
   39:   style,
   40: }: Props) => {
   41:   const sizeNum = typeof size === 'number' ? size : size === 'small' ? 24 : size === 'large' ? 40 : 32;
   42:   const palette = getAvatarPalette(name);
   43: 
   44:   const avatar = (
   45:     <Avatar
   46:       size={sizeNum}
   47:       src={avatarUrl || undefined}
   48:       icon={!avatarUrl && !name ? <UserOutlined /> : undefined}
   49:       style={{
   50:         backgroundColor: !avatarUrl ? palette.bg : undefined,
   51:         color: !avatarUrl ? palette.color : undefined,
   52:         border: !avatarUrl ? `1px solid ${palette.border}` : undefined,
   53:         fontWeight: 600,
   54:         fontSize: sizeNum >= 36 ? 14 : 12,
   55:         boxShadow: '0 1px 2px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.05)',
   56:         ...style,
   57:       }}
   58:     >
   59:       {!avatarUrl && name ? getInitials(name) : null}
   60:     </Avatar>
   61:   );
   62: 
   63:   if (!showName) return avatar;
   64: 
   65:   return (
   66:     <div style={{ display: 'inline-flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 8 }}>
   67:       {avatar}
   68:       <span style={{ fontWeight: 500, color: '#1e293b' }}>{name || '—'}</span>
   69:     </div>
   70:   );
   71: };
   72: 
   73: export default UserAvatar;
