// ============================================================
// FILE: ./src/pages/auth/ForgotPasswordPage.tsx
// ============================================================
    1: import { useState } from 'react';
    2: import { Button, Card, Form, Input, Typography, Alert, Result } from 'antd';
    3: import { MailOutlined } from '@ant-design/icons';
    4: import { Link } from 'react-router-dom';
    5: import toast from 'react-hot-toast';
    6: import { authApi } from '@/services/api/auth';
    7: import { forgotPasswordSchema } from '@/utils/validators';
    8: import type { ForgotPasswordRequest } from '@/services/types/user';
    9: 
   10: const { Title, Text } = Typography;
   11: 
   12: const ForgotPasswordPage = () => {
   13:   const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
   14:   const [sent, setSent] = useState(false);
   15:   const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
   16: 
   17:   const handleSubmit = async (values: ForgotPasswordRequest) => {
   18:     setLoading(true);
   19:     setError(null);
   20:     try {
   21:       const parsed = forgotPasswordSchema.safeParse(values);
   22:       if (!parsed.success) {
   23:         setError(parsed.error.issues[0].message);
   24:         return;
   25:       }
   26:       await authApi.forgotPassword(values);
   27:       toast.success('Đã gửi OTP đến email');
   28:       setSent(true);
   29:     } catch (err) {
   30:       const e = err as { message?: string };
   31:       setError(e?.message || 'Không thể gửi OTP');
   32:     } finally {
   33:       setLoading(false);
   34:     }
   35:   };
   36: 
   37:   return (
   38:     <div
   39:       style={{
   40:         minHeight: '100vh',
   41:         display: 'flex',
   42:         alignItems: 'center',
   43:         justifyContent: 'center',
   44:         background: 'linear-gradient(135deg, #667eea 0%, #764ba2 100%)',
   45:         padding: 24,
   46:       }}
   47:     >
   48:       <Card style={{ width: '100%', maxWidth: 420 }}>
   49:         {sent ? (
   50:           <Result
   51:             status="success"
   52:             title="Đã gửi OTP"
   53:             subTitle="Vui lòng kiểm tra email để lấy mã OTP đặt lại mật khẩu."
   54:             extra={
   55:               <Link to="/login">
   56:                 <Button type="primary">Về trang đăng nhập</Button>
   57:               </Link>
   58:             }
   59:           />
   60:         ) : (
   61:           <>
   62:             <div style={{ textAlign: 'center', marginBottom: 24 }}>
   63:               <Title level={3}>Quên mật khẩu</Title>
   64:               <Text type="secondary">Nhập email để nhận mã OTP</Text>
   65:             </div>
   66: 
   67:             {error && (
   68:               <Alert
   69:                 type="error"
   70:                 message={error}
   71:                 showIcon
   72:                 closable
   73:                 onClose={() => setError(null)}
   74:                 style={{ marginBottom: 16 }}
   75:               />
   76:             )}
   77: 
   78:             <Form layout="vertical" onFinish={handleSubmit} size="large">
   79:               <Form.Item
   80:                 name="email"
   81:                 rules={[
   82:                   { required: true, message: 'Vui lòng nhập email' },
   83:                   { type: 'email', message: 'Email không hợp lệ' },
   84:                 ]}
   85:               >
   86:                 <Input prefix={<MailOutlined />} placeholder="Email" />
   87:               </Form.Item>
   88: 
   89:               <Form.Item style={{ marginBottom: 8 }}>
   90:                 <Button type="primary" htmlType="submit" loading={loading} block>
   91:                   Gửi OTP
   92:                 </Button>
   93:               </Form.Item>
   94: 
   95:               <div style={{ textAlign: 'center' }}>
   96:                 <Link to="/login">
   97:                   <Text type="secondary" style={{ fontSize: 13 }}>
   98:                     ← Về trang đăng nhập
   99:                   </Text>
  100:                 </Link>
  101:               </div>
  102:             </Form>
  103:           </>
  104:         )}
  105:       </Card>
  106:     </div>
  107:   );
  108: };
  109: 
  110: export default ForgotPasswordPage;

// ============================================================
// FILE: ./src/pages/auth/LoginPage.tsx
// ============================================================
    1: import { useState } from 'react';
    2: import { Button, Card, Form, Input, Typography, Alert } from 'antd';
    3: import { LockOutlined, MailOutlined } from '@ant-design/icons';
    4: import { Link, useNavigate, useLocation } from 'react-router-dom';
    5: import toast from 'react-hot-toast';
    6: import { useAuth } from '@/hooks/useAuth';
    7: import { loginSchema } from '@/utils/validators';
    8: import type { LoginRequest } from '@/services/types/user';
    9: 
   10: const { Title, Text } = Typography;
   11: 
   12: const LoginPage = () => {
   13:   const { login, isAdmin, isInstructor } = useAuth();
   14:   const navigate = useNavigate();
   15:   const location = useLocation();
   16:   const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
   17:   const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
   18: 
   19:   const handleSubmit = async (values: LoginRequest) => {
   20:     setLoading(true);
   21:     setError(null);
   22:     try {
   23:       // Validate trước
   24:       const parsed = loginSchema.safeParse(values);
   25:       if (!parsed.success) {
   26:         setError(parsed.error.issues[0].message);
   27:         return;
   28:       }
   29: 
   30:       const user = await login(values);
   31:       toast.success(`Chào mừng ${user.fullName}!`);
   32: 
   33:       // Redirect theo role
   34:       const from = (location.state as { from?: { pathname: string } })?.from?.pathname;
   35:       if (from) {
   36:         navigate(from, { replace: true });
   37:       } else if (isAdmin()) {
   38:         navigate('/admin/dashboard', { replace: true });
   39:       } else if (isInstructor()) {
   40:         navigate('/instructor/dashboard', { replace: true });
   41:       } else {
   42:         navigate('/', { replace: true });
   43:       }
   44:     } catch (err) {
   45:       const e = err as { message?: string };
   46:       const message = e?.message || 'Đăng nhập thất bại';
   47:       setError(message);
   48:       toast.error(message);
   49:     } finally {
   50:       setLoading(false);
   51:     }
   52:   };
   53: 
   54:   return (
   55:     <div
   56:       style={{
   57:         minHeight: '100vh',
   58:         display: 'flex',
   59:         alignItems: 'center',
   60:         justifyContent: 'center',
   61:         background: 'linear-gradient(135deg, #667eea 0%, #764ba2 100%)',
   62:         padding: 24,
   63:       }}
   64:     >
   65:       <Card style={{ width: '100%', maxWidth: 420, boxShadow: '0 20px 60px rgba(0,0,0,0.3)' }}>
   66:         <div style={{ textAlign: 'center', marginBottom: 32 }}>
   67:           <Title level={2} style={{ marginBottom: 4 }}>
   68:             ECMS
   69:           </Title>
   70:           <Text type="secondary">Hệ thống quản lý khóa học</Text>
   71:         </div>
   72: 
   73:         {error && (
   74:           <Alert
   75:             type="error"
   76:             message={error}
   77:             showIcon
   78:             closable
   79:             onClose={() => setError(null)}
   80:             style={{ marginBottom: 16 }}
   81:           />
   82:         )}
   83: 
   84:         <Form
   85:           name="login"
   86:           layout="vertical"
   87:           onFinish={handleSubmit}
   88:           autoComplete="off"
   89:           size="large"
   90:         >
   91:           <Form.Item
   92:             name="email"
   93:             rules={[
   94:               { required: true, message: 'Vui lòng nhập email' },
   95:               { type: 'email', message: 'Email không hợp lệ' },
   96:             ]}
   97:           >
   98:             <Input prefix={<MailOutlined />} placeholder="Email" />
   99:           </Form.Item>
  100: 
  101:           <Form.Item
  102:             name="password"
  103:             rules={[{ required: true, message: 'Vui lòng nhập mật khẩu' }]}
  104:           >
  105:             <Input.Password prefix={<LockOutlined />} placeholder="Mật khẩu" />
  106:           </Form.Item>
  107: 
  108:           <div style={{ textAlign: 'right', marginBottom: 16 }}>
  109:             <Link to="/forgot-password">
  110:               <Text type="secondary" style={{ fontSize: 13 }}>
  111:                 Quên mật khẩu?
  112:               </Text>
  113:             </Link>
  114:           </div>
  115: 
  116:           <Form.Item style={{ marginBottom: 0 }}>
  117:             <Button type="primary" htmlType="submit" loading={loading} block>
  118:               Đăng nhập
  119:             </Button>
  120:           </Form.Item>
  121:         </Form>
  122: 
  123:         <div style={{ textAlign: 'center', marginTop: 24 }}>
  124:           <Text type="secondary" style={{ fontSize: 12 }}>
  125:             © 2026 ECMS Web Admin
  126:           </Text>
  127:         </div>
  128:       </Card>
  129:     </div>
  130:   );
  131: };
  132: 
  133: export default LoginPage;

// ============================================================
// FILE: ./src/pages/auth/RegisterPage.tsx
// ============================================================
    1: import { useState } from 'react';
    2: import { Button, Card, Checkbox, Form, Input, Typography, Alert } from 'antd';
    3: import { LockOutlined, MailOutlined, PhoneOutlined, UserOutlined } from '@ant-design/icons';
    4: import { Link, useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
    5: import toast from 'react-hot-toast';
    6: import { authApi } from '@/services/api/auth';
    7: import { registerSchema } from '@/utils/validators';
    8: import type { RegisterRequest } from '@/services/types/user';
    9: 
   10: const { Title, Text } = Typography;
   11: const RegisterPage = () => {
   12:   const navigate = useNavigate(); const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false); const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
   13:   const handleSubmit = async (values: RegisterRequest) => {
   14:     setLoading(true); setError(null);
   15:     try {
   16:       const parsed = registerSchema.safeParse(values);
   17:       if (!parsed.success) { setError(parsed.error.issues[0].message); return; }
   18:       await authApi.register(values); toast.success('Đăng ký thành công!'); navigate('/login', { replace: true });
   19:     } catch (err) { setError((err as any)?.message || 'Lỗi'); toast.error('Đăng ký thất bại'); } finally { setLoading(false); }
   20:   };
   21:   return (
   22:     <div style={{ minHeight: '100vh', display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', background: 'linear-gradient(135deg, #667eea 0%, #764ba2 100%)', padding: 24 }}>
   23:       <Card style={{ width: '100%', maxWidth: 460 }}>
   24:         <div style={{ textAlign: 'center', marginBottom: 24 }}><Title level={2}>Đăng ký</Title><Text type="secondary">Tạo tài khoản ECMS</Text></div>
   25:         {error && <Alert type="error" message={error} showIcon closable style={{ marginBottom: 16 }} />}
   26:         <Form layout="vertical" onFinish={handleSubmit} size="large">
   27:           <Form.Item name="fullName" rules={[{ required: true, message: 'Nhập họ tên' }]}><Input prefix={<UserOutlined />} placeholder="Họ và tên" /></Form.Item>
   28:           <Form.Item name="email" rules={[{ required: true, type: 'email' }]}><Input prefix={<MailOutlined />} placeholder="Email" /></Form.Item>
   29:           <Form.Item name="phone"><Input prefix={<PhoneOutlined />} placeholder="Số điện thoại" /></Form.Item>
   30:           <Form.Item name="password" rules={[{ required: true, min: 6 }]}><Input.Password prefix={<LockOutlined />} placeholder="Mật khẩu" /></Form.Item>
   31:           <Form.Item name="confirmPassword" dependencies={['password']} rules={[{ required: true }, ({ getFieldValue }) => ({ validator(_, v) { if (!v || getFieldValue('password') === v) return Promise.resolve(); return Promise.reject('Không khớp'); } })]}><Input.Password prefix={<LockOutlined />} placeholder="Xác nhận mật khẩu" /></Form.Item>
   32:           <Form.Item name="agreedToTerms" valuePropName="checked" rules={[{ validator: (_, v) => v ? Promise.resolve() : Promise.reject('Đồng ý điều khoản') }]}><Checkbox>Tôi đồng ý điều khoản</Checkbox></Form.Item>
   33:           <Form.Item><Button type="primary" htmlType="submit" loading={loading} block>Đăng ký</Button></Form.Item>
   34:           <div style={{ textAlign: 'center' }}><Text type="secondary">Đã có tài khoản? </Text><Link to="/login">Đăng nhập</Link></div>
   35:         </Form>
   36:       </Card>
   37:     </div>
   38:   );
   39: };
   40: export default RegisterPage;

// FILE: ./src/pages/ForbiddenPage.tsx

    1: import { Button, Result } from 'antd';
    2: import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
    3: 
    4: const ForbiddenPage = () => {
    5:   const navigate = useNavigate();
    6: 
    7:   return (
    8:     <div
    9:       style={{
   10:         minHeight: '100vh',
   11:         display: 'flex',
   12:         alignItems: 'center',
   13:         justifyContent: 'center',
   14:       }}
   15:     >
   16:       <Result
   17:         status="403"
   18:         title="403"
   19:         subTitle="Bạn không có quyền truy cập trang này."
   20:         extra={
   21:           <Button type="primary" onClick={() => navigate('/')}>
   22:             Về trang chủ
   23:           </Button>
   24:         }

// ============================================================
// FILE: ./src/pages/landing/CourseCatalogPage.tsx
// ============================================================
    1: import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
    2: import { Button, Card, Col, Empty, Row, Spin, Tag, Typography } from 'antd';
    3: import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
    4: import toast from 'react-hot-toast';
    5: import { courseApi } from '@/services/api/courses';
    6: import MoneyDisplay from '@/components/common/MoneyDisplay';
    7: import type { CourseResponse } from '@/services/types/course';
    8: const { Title, Paragraph, Text } = Typography;
    9: const CourseCatalogPage = () => {
   10:   const navigate = useNavigate(); const [courses, setCourses] = useState<CourseResponse[]>([]); const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
   11:   useEffect(() => { courseApi.search({ page: 0, size: 20, status: 'PUBLISHED' }).then((res) => setCourses(res.content)).catch(() => toast.error('Lỗi tải khóa học')).finally(() => setLoading(false)); }, []);
   12:   if (loading) return <div style={{ textAlign: 'center', padding: 64 }}><Spin size="large" /></div>;
   13:   return (
   14:     <div style={{ padding: '48px 24px', maxWidth: 1200, margin: '0 auto' }}>
   15:       <Title level={2}>Danh mục khóa học</Title>
   16:       <Paragraph type="secondary" style={{ marginBottom: 32 }}>Khám phá các khóa học chất lượng cao.</Paragraph>
   17:       {courses.length === 0 ? <Empty /> : (
   18:         <Row gutter={[24, 24]}>
   19:           {courses.map((c) => (<Col xs={24} sm={12} lg={8} key={c.id}><Card hoverable cover={c.thumbnailUrl ? <img src={c.thumbnailUrl} style={{ height: 180, objectFit: 'cover' }} /> : undefined} onClick={() => navigate('/login')}><Tag color="cyan">{c.courseType}</Tag><Title level={5} ellipsis={{ rows: 2 }}>{c.title}</Title><Paragraph type="secondary" ellipsis={{ rows: 2 }}>{c.shortDescription}</Paragraph><Text strong style={{ color: '#1677ff' }}><MoneyDisplay value={c.isFree ? 0 : c.price} /></Text></Card></Col>))}
   20:         </Row>
   21:       )}
   22:     </div>
   23:   );
   24: };
   25: export default CourseCatalogPage;

// ============================================================
// FILE: ./src/pages/ProfilePage.tsx
// ============================================================
    1: import { useState } from 'react';
    2: import {
    3:   Card, Form, Input, Button, Tabs, Space, Avatar, Typography,
    4:   Tag, Divider, message,
    5: } from 'antd';
    6: import { UserOutlined, LockOutlined, SaveOutlined, MailOutlined, PhoneOutlined, HomeOutlined } from '@ant-design/icons';
    7: import toast from 'react-hot-toast';
    8: 
    9: import PageContainer from '@/components/common/PageContainer';
   10: import PageHeader from '@/components/common/PageHeader';
   11: import { authApi } from '@/services/api/auth';
   12: import { useAuth } from '@/hooks/useAuth';
   13: import { ROLE_LABEL } from '@/constants/roles';
   14: import type { UpdateProfileRequest, ChangePasswordRequest } from '@/services/types/user';
   15: 
   16: const { Title, Text } = Typography;
   17: 
   18: const ProfilePage = () => {
   19:   const { user, setUser } = useAuth();
   20:   const [profileForm] = Form.useForm<UpdateProfileRequest>();
   21:   const [passwordForm] = Form.useForm<ChangePasswordRequest>();
   22:   const [loadingProfile, setLoadingProfile] = useState(false);
   23:   const [loadingPassword, setLoadingPassword] = useState(false);
   24: 
   25:   // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   26:   // Update profile
   27:   // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   28:   const handleUpdateProfile = async (values: UpdateProfileRequest) => {
   29:     setLoadingProfile(true);
   30:     try {
   31:       const updated = await authApi.updateProfile(values);
   32:       setUser(updated);
   33:       toast.success('Cập nhật thông tin thành công');
   34:     } catch (err) {
   35:       const e = err as { message?: string };
   36:       toast.error(e?.message || 'Cập nhật thất bại');
   37:     } finally {
   38:       setLoadingProfile(false);
   39:     }
   40:   };
   41: 
   42:   // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   43:   // Change password
   44:   // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   45:   const handleChangePassword = async (values: ChangePasswordRequest) => {
   46:     if (values.newPassword !== values.confirmPassword) {
   47:       message.error('Mật khẩu xác nhận không khớp');
   48:       return;
   49:     }
   50:     setLoadingPassword(true);
   51:     try {
   52:       await authApi.changePassword(values);
   53:       toast.success('Đổi mật khẩu thành công. Vui lòng đăng nhập lại.');
   54:       passwordForm.resetFields();
   55:       // Backend revoke all sessions → redirect login sau 2s
   56:       setTimeout(() => {
   57:         window.location.href = '/login';
   58:       }, 2000);
   59:     } catch (err) {
   60:       const e = err as { message?: string };
   61:       toast.error(e?.message || 'Đổi mật khẩu thất bại');
   62:     } finally {
   63:       setLoadingPassword(false);
   64:     }
   65:   };
   66: 
   67:   // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   68:   // Render
   69:   // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   70:   return (
   71:     <PageContainer>
   72:       <PageHeader
   73:         title="Hồ sơ cá nhân"
   74:         subtitle="Xem và cập nhật thông tin tài khoản của bạn"
   75:       />
   76: 
   77:       {/* Info card */}
   78:       <Card style={{ marginBottom: 24 }}>
   79:         <Space size={24} align="center">
   80:           <Avatar
   81:             size={80}
   82:             src={user?.avatarUrl || undefined}
   83:             icon={!user?.avatarUrl ? <UserOutlined /> : undefined}
   84:             style={{ backgroundColor: '#1677ff' }}
   85:           />
   86:           <div>
   87:             <Title level={4} style={{ margin: 0 }}>
   88:               {user?.fullName || 'Chưa cập nhật'}
   89:             </Title>
   90:             <Space style={{ marginTop: 4 }}>
   91:               <MailOutlined /> <Text>{user?.email}</Text>
   92:             </Space>
   93:             <div style={{ marginTop: 8 }}>
   94:               <Tag color="blue">{user?.roleCode ? ROLE_LABEL[user.roleCode] : ''}</Tag>
   95:             </div>
   96:           </div>
   97:         </Space>
   98:       </Card>
   99: 
  100:       {/* Tabs */}
  101:       <Card>
  102:         <Tabs
  103:           defaultActiveKey="profile"
  104:           items={[
  105:             {
  106:               key: 'profile',
  107:               label: (
  108:                 <span>
  109:                   <UserOutlined /> Thông tin cá nhân
  110:                 </span>
  111:               ),
  112:               children: (
  113:                 <Form
  114:                   form={profileForm}
  115:                   layout="vertical"
  116:                   initialValues={{
  117:                     fullName: user?.fullName,
  118:                     phone: user?.phone,
  119:                     address: user?.address,
  120:                     avatarUrl: user?.avatarUrl,
  121:                   }}
  122:                   onFinish={handleUpdateProfile}
  123:                   style={{ maxWidth: 600 }}
  124:                 >
  125:                   <Form.Item
  126:                     name="fullName"
  127:                     label="Họ và tên"
  128:                     rules={[
  129:                       { required: true, message: 'Vui lòng nhập họ tên' },
  130:                       { max: 150, message: 'Tối đa 150 ký tự' },
  131:                     ]}
  132:                   >
  133:                     <Input prefix={<UserOutlined />} placeholder="Nguyễn Văn A" />
  134:                   </Form.Item>
  135: 
  136:                   <Form.Item
  137:                     name="phone"
  138:                     label="Số điện thoại"
  139:                     rules={[
  140:                       { pattern: /^\d{10,11}$/, message: 'SĐT phải 10-11 chữ số' },
  141:                     ]}
  142:                   >
  143:                     <Input prefix={<PhoneOutlined />} placeholder="0901234567" />
  144:                   </Form.Item>
  145: 
  146:                   <Form.Item
  147:                     name="address"
  148:                     label="Địa chỉ"
  149:                     rules={[{ max: 255, message: 'Tối đa 255 ký tự' }]}
  150:                   >
  151:                     <Input prefix={<HomeOutlined />} placeholder="Hà Nội" />
  152:                   </Form.Item>
  153: 
  154:                   <Form.Item
  155:                     name="avatarUrl"
  156:                     label="URL ảnh đại diện"
  157:                     rules={[{ max: 500, message: 'Tối đa 500 ký tự' }]}
  158:                   >
  159:                     <Input placeholder="https://..." />
  160:                   </Form.Item>
  161: 
  162:                   <Form.Item>
  163:                     <Button
  164:                       type="primary"
  165:                       htmlType="submit"
  166:                       icon={<SaveOutlined />}
  167:                       loading={loadingProfile}
  168:                     >
  169:                       Lưu thay đổi
  170:                     </Button>
  171:                   </Form.Item>
  172:                 </Form>
  173:               ),
  174:             },
  175:             {
  176:               key: 'password',
  177:               label: (
  178:                 <span>
  179:                   <LockOutlined /> Đổi mật khẩu
  180:                 </span>
  181:               ),
  182:               children: (
  183:                 <Form
  184:                   form={passwordForm}
  185:                   layout="vertical"
  186:                   onFinish={handleChangePassword}
  187:                   style={{ maxWidth: 600 }}
  188:                 >
  189:                   <Form.Item
  190:                     name="oldPassword"
  191:                     label="Mật khẩu hiện tại"
  192:                     rules={[{ required: true, message: 'Vui lòng nhập mật khẩu cũ' }]}
  193:                   >
  194:                     <Input.Password prefix={<LockOutlined />} placeholder="••••••" />
  195:                   </Form.Item>
  196: 
  197:                   <Form.Item
  198:                     name="newPassword"
  199:                     label="Mật khẩu mới"
  200:                     rules={[
  201:                       { required: true, message: 'Vui lòng nhập mật khẩu mới' },
  202:                       { min: 6, message: 'Ít nhất 6 ký tự' },
  203:                       { max: 100, message: 'Tối đa 100 ký tự' },
  204:                     ]}
  205:                   >
  206:                     <Input.Password prefix={<LockOutlined />} placeholder="••••••" />
  207:                   </Form.Item>
  208: 
  209:                   <Form.Item
  210:                     name="confirmPassword"
  211:                     label="Xác nhận mật khẩu mới"
  212:                     dependencies={['newPassword']}
  213:                     rules={[
  214:                       { required: true, message: 'Vui lòng xác nhận mật khẩu' },
  215:                       ({ getFieldValue }) => ({
  216:                         validator(_, value) {
  217:                           if (!value || getFieldValue('newPassword') === value) {
  218:                             return Promise.resolve();
  219:                           }
  220:                           return Promise.reject(new Error('Mật khẩu xác nhận không khớp'));
  221:                         },
  222:                       }),
  223:                     ]}
  224:                   >
  225:                     <Input.Password prefix={<LockOutlined />} placeholder="••••••" />
  226:                   </Form.Item>
  227: 
  228:                   <Divider />
  229: 
  230:                   <Form.Item>
  231:                     <Button
  232:                       type="primary"
  233:                       htmlType="submit"
  234:                       icon={<SaveOutlined />}
  235:                       loading={loadingPassword}
  236:                       danger
  237:                     >
  238:                       Đổi mật khẩu
  239:                     </Button>
  240:                     <Text type="secondary" style={{ marginLeft: 12, fontSize: 12 }}>
  241:                       Sau khi đổi, bạn sẽ bị đăng xuất khỏi tất cả thiết bị.
  242:                     </Text>
  243:                   </Form.Item>
  244:                 </Form>
  245:               ),
  246:             },
  247:           ]}
  248:         />
  249:       </Card>
  250:     </PageContainer>
  251:   );
  252: };
  253: 
  254: export default ProfilePage;

// ============================================================
// FILE: ./src/pages/student/dashboard/StudentDashboardPage.tsx
// ============================================================
    1: import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
    2: import { Card, Col, Row, Spin, Statistic } from 'antd';
    3: import { BookOutlined, ProfileOutlined, CheckCircleOutlined, TrophyOutlined } from '@ant-design/icons';
    4: import toast from 'react-hot-toast';
    5: import PageContainer from '@/components/common/PageContainer';
    6: import PageHeader from '@/components/common/PageHeader';
    7: import { useAuth } from '@/hooks/useAuth';
    8: import { progressApi } from '@/services/api/courses';
    9: const StudentDashboardPage = () => {
   10:   const { user } = useAuth(); const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true); const [stats, setStats] = useState({ total: 0, inProgress: 0, completed: 0 });
   11:   useEffect(() => { progressApi.getMyCourses({ page: 0, size: 100 }).then((res) => { const list = res.content || []; setStats({ total: list.length, inProgress: list.filter(p => p.status === 'IN_PROGRESS').length, completed: list.filter(p => p.status === 'COMPLETED').length }); }).catch(() => toast.error('Lỗi tải dữ liệu')).finally(() => setLoading(false)); }, []);
   12:   return (
   13:     <PageContainer>
   14:       <PageHeader title={`Xin chào, ${user?.fullName || 'Học viên'} 👋`} subtitle="Tổng quan việc học" />
   15:       {loading ? <div style={{ textAlign: 'center', padding: 64 }}><Spin size="large" /></div> : (
   16:         <Row gutter={[16, 16]}>
   17:           <Col xs={24} sm={12} lg={6}><Card><Statistic title="Đã đăng ký" value={stats.total} prefix={<BookOutlined />} valueStyle={{ color: '#1677ff' }} /></Card></Col>
   18:           <Col xs={24} sm={12} lg={6}><Card><Statistic title="Đang học" value={stats.inProgress} prefix={<ProfileOutlined />} valueStyle={{ color: '#faad14' }} /></Card></Col>
   19:           <Col xs={24} sm={12} lg={6}><Card><Statistic title="Hoàn thành" value={stats.completed} prefix={<CheckCircleOutlined />} valueStyle={{ color: '#52c41a' }} /></Card></Col>
   20:           <Col xs={24} sm={12} lg={6}><Card><Statistic title="Điểm TB" value="—" prefix={<TrophyOutlined />} valueStyle={{ color: '#eb2f96' }} /></Card></Col>
   21:         </Row>
   22:       )}
   23:     </PageContainer>
   24:   );
   25: };

// FILE: ./src/pages/student/my-courses/CourseLearnPage.tsx
// ============================================================
    1: import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
    2: import { useParams } from 'react-router-dom';
    3: import { Spin } from 'antd';
    4: import toast from 'react-hot-toast';
    5: import PageContainer from '@/components/common/PageContainer';
    6: import PageHeader from '@/components/common/PageHeader';
    7: import ErrorState from '@/components/feedback/ErrorState';
    8: import CourseOverviewCard from '@/components/course/CourseOverviewCard';
    9: import CourseResourceTree from '@/components/course/CourseResourceTree';
   10: import { courseApi } from '@/services/api/courses';
   11: import type { CourseDetailResponse } from '@/services/types/course';
   12: const CourseLearnPage = () => {
   13:   const { id } = useParams<{ id: string }>(); const courseId = Number(id);
   14:   const [course, setCourse] = useState<CourseDetailResponse | null>(null); const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true); const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
   15:   useEffect(() => { if (!courseId || isNaN(courseId)) { setError('ID lỗi'); setLoading(false); return; } courseApi.getById(courseId).then(setCourse).catch((e) => { setError(e?.message || 'Lỗi'); toast.error('Lỗi'); }).finally(() => setLoading(false)); }, [courseId]);
   16:   if (loading) return <div style={{ textAlign: 'center', padding: 64 }}><Spin size="large" /></div>;
   17:   if (error || !course) return <ErrorState description={error || 'Không tìm thấy'} />;
   18:   return (<PageContainer><PageHeader title={course.title} subtitle={course.code} backButton backTo="/student/courses" /><div style={{ display: 'grid', gap: 24 }}><CourseOverviewCard course={course} /><CourseResourceTree resources={course.resources || []} /></div></PageContainer>);
   19: };
   20: export default CourseLearnPage;

// ============================================================
// FILE: ./src/pages/student/my-courses/MyCoursesPage.tsx
// ============================================================
    1: import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
    2: import { Button, Card, Col, Empty, Progress, Row, Spin, Typography } from 'antd';
    3: import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
    4: import toast from 'react-hot-toast';
    5: import PageContainer from '@/components/common/PageContainer';
    6: import PageHeader from '@/components/common/PageHeader';
    7: import { progressApi } from '@/services/api/courses';
    8: import type { ProgressResponse } from '@/services/types/course';
    9: const { Title, Text } = Typography;
   10: const MyCoursesPage = () => {
   11:   const navigate = useNavigate(); const [items, setItems] = useState<ProgressResponse[]>([]); const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
   12:   useEffect(() => { progressApi.getMyCourses({ page: 0, size: 50 }).then((res) => setItems(res.content || [])).catch(() => toast.error('Lỗi')).finally(() => setLoading(false)); }, []);
   13:   return (
   14:     <PageContainer>
   15:       <PageHeader title="Khóa học của tôi" subtitle={`Tổng ${items.length}`} />
   16:       {loading ? <div style={{ textAlign: 'center', padding: 64 }}><Spin size="large" /></div> : items.length === 0 ? (
   17:         <Empty description="Chưa đăng ký"><Button type="primary" onClick={() => navigate('/courses')}>Khám phá</Button></Empty>
   18:       ) : (
   19:         <Row gutter={[16, 16]}>
   20:           {items.map((p) => (<Col xs={24} sm={12} lg={8} key={p.id}><Card hoverable onClick={() => navigate(`/student/courses/${p.courseId}/learn`)}><Title level={5} ellipsis={{ rows: 2 }}>{p.courseTitle || `Khóa #${p.courseId}`}</Title><Progress percent={Math.round(p.progressPercentage || 0)} /><Text type="secondary" style={{ fontSize: 12 }}>{p.status}</Text></Card></Col>))}
   21:         </Row>
   22:       )}
   23:     </PageContainer>
   24:   );
   25: };
   26: export default MyCoursesPage;

// ============================================================
// FILE: ./src/routes/ProtectedRoute.tsx
// ============================================================
    1: import { Navigate, Outlet, useLocation } from 'react-router-dom';
    2: import { useAuth } from '@/hooks/useAuth';
    3: import { ROLE } from '@/constants/roles';
    4: import PageLoading from '@/components/feedback/PageLoading';
    5: 
    6: interface Props {
    7:   children?: React.ReactNode;
    8:   allowedRoles?: string[];
    9: }
   10: 
   11: const ProtectedRoute = ({ children, allowedRoles }: Props) => {
   12:   const { user, isAuthenticated, initialized } = useAuth();
   13:   const location = useLocation();
   14: 
   15:   // Chưa init xong → chờ
   16:   if (!initialized) {
   17:     return <PageLoading tip="Đang kiểm tra đăng nhập..." />;
   18:   }
   19: 
   20:   // Chưa login → redirect login
   21:   if (!isAuthenticated) {
   22:     return <Navigate to="/login" replace state={{ from: location }} />;
   23:   }
   24: 
   25:   // Sai role → 403
   26:   if (allowedRoles && allowedRoles.length > 0) {
   27:     const userRole = user?.roleCode;
   28:     if (!userRole || !allowedRoles.includes(userRole)) {
   29:       return <Navigate to="/403" replace />;
   30:     }
   31:   }
   32: 
   33:   return children ? <>{children}</> : <Outlet />;
   34: };
   35: 
   36: export default ProtectedRoute;
   37: 
   38: export const ADMIN_ROLES = [ROLE.ADMIN];
   39: export const INSTRUCTOR_ROLES = [ROLE.ADMIN, ROLE.INSTRUCTOR];
   40: export const STUDENT_ROLES = [ROLE.STUDENT];

// ============================================================
// FILE: ./src/routes/PublicRoute.tsx
// ============================================================
    1: import { Navigate, Outlet } from 'react-router-dom';
    2: import { useAuth } from '@/hooks/useAuth';
    3: import PageLoading from '@/components/feedback/PageLoading';
    4: const PublicRoute = ({ redirectIfAuthenticated = false, redirectTo = '/student/dashboard' }) => {
    5:   const { isAuthenticated, initialized } = useAuth();
    6:   if (!initialized) return <PageLoading tip="Đang kiểm tra..." />;
    7:   if (redirectIfAuthenticated && isAuthenticated) return <Navigate to={redirectTo} replace />;
    8:   return <Outlet />;
    9: };
   10: export default PublicRoute;

    3: const StudentRoute = () => (<ProtectedRoute allowedRoles={STUDENT_ROLES}><Outlet /></ProtectedRoute>);
    4: export default StudentRoute;

   13: export const adminUserApi = {
   14:   search: async (params: Record<string, unknown>): Promise<PageResponse<AccountResponse>> => {
   15:     const { data } = await api.get<RequestResponse<PageResponse<AccountResponse>>>(
   16:       API_ENDPOINT.RBAC_ACCOUNTS,
   17:       { params }
   18:     );
   19:     return data.data;
   20:   },
   21:

   23:     const { data } = await api.get<RequestResponse<AccountResponse>>(
   24:       API_ENDPOINT.RBAC_ACCOUNT_DETAIL(id)
   25:     );
   26:     return data.data;
   27:   },
   28:

   30:     const { data } = await api.get<RequestResponse<AccountRoleResponse[]>>(
   31:       API_ENDPOINT.RBAC_ACCOUNT_ROLES(id)
   32:     );
   33:     return data.data;
   34:   },

   45:   },
   46: };

   55:     return data.data;

// ============================================================
// FILE: ./src/services/api/axiosConfig.ts
// ============================================================
    1: // src/services/api/axiosConfig.ts
    2: import axios from 'axios';
    3: import type { AxiosError, AxiosInstance, InternalAxiosRequestConfig } from 'axios';
    4: import { API_ENDPOINT } from '@/constants/api-endpoint';
    5: 
    6: // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    7: // EXTEND AXIOS ERROR TYPES
    8: // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    9: export interface AppAxiosError extends AxiosError {
   10:   isSilent?: boolean;
   11:   isAuthError?: boolean;
   12:   isSessionExpired?: boolean;
   13:   isBusinessError?: boolean;
   14: }
   15: 
   16: type RetryConfig = InternalAxiosRequestConfig & { _retry?: boolean };
   17: 
   18: interface ApiResponse<T = unknown> {
   19:   status?: string;
   20:   message?: string;
   21:   error?: string;
   22:   data?: T;
   23: }
   24: 
   25: interface QueueItem {
   26:   resolve: () => void;
   27:   reject: (reason?: unknown) => void;
   28: }
   29: // http://localhost:8080
   30: // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   31: // CONFIG
   32: // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   33: export const API_HOST = import.meta.env.VITE_API_URL || '';
   34: 
   35: /** ⚠️ KHÔNG append `/api` — endpoint đã có prefix này */
   36: export const API_BASE_URL = API_HOST;
   37: 
   38: export const APP_CONFIG = {
   39:   API_BASE_URL: API_HOST,
   40:   API_TIMEOUT: 30000,
   41:   REFRESH_TIMEOUT: 10000,
   42: } as const;
   43: 
   44: // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   45: // CREATE INSTANCE
   46: // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   47: const api: AxiosInstance = axios.create({
   48:   baseURL: API_BASE_URL,
   49:   timeout: APP_CONFIG.API_TIMEOUT,
   50:   withCredentials: true, // ✅ Cookie-based auth khớp BE
   51:   headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
   52: });
   53: 
   54: // Auto-bỏ Content-Type khi gửi FormData (multipart)
   55: api.interceptors.request.use((config: InternalAxiosRequestConfig) => {
   56:   if (config.data instanceof FormData) {
   57:     delete config.headers['Content-Type'];
   58:   }
   59:   return config;
   60: });
   61: 
   62: // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   63: // DEBUG
   64: // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   65: const DEBUG = import.meta.env.DEV;
   66: const log = (...args: unknown[]) => {
   67:   if (DEBUG) console.log(...args);
   68: };
   69: 
   70: // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   71: // STATE
   72: // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   73: let isRefreshing = false;
   74: let failedQueue: QueueItem[] = [];
   75: 
   76: // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   77: // URL LISTS
   78: // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   79: const NO_REFRESH_URLS: string[] = [
   80:   API_ENDPOINT.AUTH_LOGIN,
   81:   API_ENDPOINT.AUTH_REGISTER,
   82:   API_ENDPOINT.AUTH_REFRESH_TOKEN,
   83:   API_ENDPOINT.AUTH_LOGOUT,
   84:   API_ENDPOINT.AUTH_FORGOT_PASSWORD,
   85:   API_ENDPOINT.AUTH_VERIFY_OTP,
   86:   API_ENDPOINT.AUTH_RESET_PASSWORD,
   87: ];
   88: 
   89: const SILENT_URLS: string[] = [API_ENDPOINT.AUTH_REFRESH_TOKEN];
   90: 
   91: // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   92: // SESSION EXPIRED EMITTER
   93: // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   94: type SessionExpiredListener = () => void;
   95: const sessionExpiredListeners = new Set<SessionExpiredListener>();
   96: 
   97: export const subscribeSessionExpired = (
   98:   listener: SessionExpiredListener
   99: ): (() => void) => {
  100:   sessionExpiredListeners.add(listener);
  101:   return () => {
  102:     sessionExpiredListeners.delete(listener);
  103:   };
  104: };
  105: 
  106: const emitSessionExpired = () => {
  107:   sessionExpiredListeners.forEach((fn) => {
  108:     try {
  109:       fn();
  110:     } catch (e) {
  111:       console.warn('[axiosConfig] sessionExpired listener error:', e);
  112:     }
  113:   });
  114: };
  115: 
  116: // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  117: // HELPERS
  118: // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  119: const processQueue = (error: unknown) => {
  120:   failedQueue.forEach((p) => {
  121:     if (error) p.reject(error);
  122:     else p.resolve();
  123:   });
  124:   failedQueue = [];
  125: };
  126: 
  127: const extractErrorMessage = (error: AppAxiosError): string => {
  128:   if (!error.response) {
  129:     return 'Không thể kết nối đến server. Vui lòng kiểm tra kết nối mạng.';
  130:   }
  131: 
  132:   const data = error.response.data as ApiResponse | undefined;
  133:   if (data?.message) return data.message;
  134:   if (data?.error) return data.error;
  135: 
  136:   const statusMessages: Record<number, string> = {
  137:     400: 'Yêu cầu không hợp lệ',
  138:     401: 'Phiên đăng nhập đã hết hạn',
  139:     403: 'Bạn không có quyền truy cập',
  140:     404: 'Không tìm thấy tài nguyên',
  141:     409: 'Dữ liệu đã tồn tại',
  142:     423: 'Tài khoản đang bị khóa',
  143:     429: 'Quá nhiều yêu cầu, vui lòng thử lại sau',
  144:     500: 'Lỗi hệ thống, vui lòng thử lại sau',
  145:   };
  146: 
  147:   return statusMessages[error.response.status] || 'Có lỗi xảy ra';
  148: };
  149: 
  150: const shouldSkipRefresh = (url?: string): boolean =>
  151:   url ? NO_REFRESH_URLS.some((e) => url.includes(e)) : false;
  152: 
  153: const shouldBeSilent = (url?: string): boolean =>
  154:   url ? SILENT_URLS.some((e) => url.includes(e)) : false;
  155: 
  156: // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  157: // REQUEST INTERCEPTOR
  158: // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  159: api.interceptors.request.use(
  160:   (config: InternalAxiosRequestConfig) => {
  161:     if (!shouldBeSilent(config.url)) {
  162:       log('🔵 Request:', config.method?.toUpperCase(), config.url);
  163:     }
  164:     return config;
  165:   },
  166:   (error: AxiosError) => {
  167:     log('❌ Request Error:', error.message);
  168:     return Promise.reject(error);
  169:   }
  170: );
  171: 
  172: // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  173: // RESPONSE INTERCEPTOR
  174: // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  175: api.interceptors.response.use(
  176:   // ── Success handler ──
  177:   (response) => {
  178:     if (!shouldBeSilent(response.config.url)) {
  179:       log('🟢 Response:', response.status, response.config.url);
  180:     }
  181: 
  182:     // BE trả { status: 'error' } dù HTTP 200 → reject
  183:     const data = response.data as ApiResponse | undefined;
  184:     if (data?.status === 'error') {
  185:       const err = new Error(
  186:         data.message || data.error || 'Có lỗi xảy ra từ server'
  187:       ) as AppAxiosError;
  188:       err.response = response;
  189:       err.isBusinessError = true;
  190:       err.isSilent = shouldBeSilent(response.config.url);
  191:       return Promise.reject(err);
  192:     }
  193: 
  194:     return response;
  195:   },
  196: 
  197:   // ── Error handler ──
  198:   async (error: AxiosError) => {
  199:     const appErr = error as AppAxiosError;
  200:     const originalRequest = appErr.config as RetryConfig | undefined;
  201:     const silent = shouldBeSilent(originalRequest?.url);
  202: 
  203:     if (appErr.response) {
  204:       appErr.message = extractErrorMessage(appErr);
  205:     }
  206: 
  207:     // ── Silent URL fail 401 ──
  208:     if (silent && appErr.response?.status === 401) {
  209:       appErr.isSilent = true;
  210:       appErr.isAuthError = true;
  211:       appErr.isSessionExpired = true;
  212:       emitSessionExpired();
  213:       return Promise.reject(appErr);
  214:     }
  215: 
  216:     // ── Không phải 401, hoặc đã retry ──
  217:     if (
  218:       appErr.response?.status !== 401 ||
  219:       !originalRequest ||
  220:       originalRequest._retry
  221:     ) {
  222:       return Promise.reject(appErr);
  223:     }
  224: 
  225:     // ── Endpoint không được refresh ──
  226:     if (shouldSkipRefresh(originalRequest.url)) {
  227:       return Promise.reject(appErr);
  228:     }
  229: 
  230:     // ── Đang refresh → queue ──
  231:     if (isRefreshing) {
  232:       originalRequest._retry = true; // 🆕 tránh loop vô tận
  233:       log('⏳ Đang refresh, queue request:', originalRequest.url);
  234: 
  235:       return new Promise<void>((resolve, reject) => {
  236:         failedQueue.push({ resolve, reject });
  237:       })
  238:         .then(() => {
  239:           log('🔄 Retry từ queue:', originalRequest.url);
  240:           return api(originalRequest);
  241:         })
  242:         .catch((err) => Promise.reject(err));
  243:     }
  244: 
  245:     // ── Bắt đầu refresh ──
  246:     originalRequest._retry = true;
  247:     isRefreshing = true;
  248: 
  249:     try {
  250:       log('⚠️ Access token hết hạn — gọi refresh-token...');
  251:       await api.post(API_ENDPOINT.AUTH_REFRESH_TOKEN, null, {
  252:         timeout: APP_CONFIG.REFRESH_TIMEOUT,
  253:       });
  254:       log('✅ Refresh thành công, cookie mới đã set');
  255: 
  256:       processQueue(null);
  257:       isRefreshing = false;
  258: 
  259:       return api(originalRequest);
  260:     } catch (refreshError: unknown) {
  261:       const err = refreshError as AppAxiosError;
  262:       const isAuthFail = err?.response?.status === 401;
  263: 
  264:       if (isAuthFail) {
  265:         err.isSilent = true;
  266:         err.isAuthError = true;
  267:         err.isSessionExpired = true;
  268:         emitSessionExpired();
  269:       } else {
  270:         log('❌ Refresh thất bại:', err?.message);
  271:       }
  272: 
  273:       processQueue(refreshError);
  274:       isRefreshing = false;
  275: 
  276:       return Promise.reject(refreshError);
  277:     }
  278:   }
  279: );
  280: 
  281: export default api;

   16: // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   17: // HTTP helper — unwrap RequestResponse
   18: // Response BE: { data: <payload>, message: "OK" }
   19: // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   20: const API = '/api/chat';
   21: 
   22: type PageResult<T> = { content?: T[] };
   23: 
   24: const unwrap = <T,>(res: { data?: unknown }): T => {
   25:   const body = res?.data;
   26:   if (body && typeof body === 'object') {
   27:     if ('data' in body) return (body as { data: T }).data;
   28:     if ('result' in body) return (body as { result: T }).result;
   29:   }
   30:   return body as T;
   31: };
   32: 
   33: // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   34: // CONVERSATIONS
   35: // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   36: export const getMyConversations = async (
   37:   page = 0,
   38:   size = 20,
   39: ): Promise<Conversation[]> => {
   40:   const res = await api.get(`${API}/conversations`, { params: { page, size } });
   41:   const pageData = unwrap<PageResult<Conversation>>(res);
   42:   return pageData?.content ?? [];
   43: };
   44: 
   45: export const getConversationById = async (id: number): Promise<Conversation> => {
   46:   const res = await api.get(`${API}/conversations/${id}`);
   47:   return unwrap<Conversation>(res);
   48: };

  177:     const { data } = await api.put<RequestResponse<CourseResourceResponse>>(
  178:       API_ENDPOINT.RESOURCE_DETAIL(courseId, id),
  179:       payload,
  180:     );
  181:     return data.data;
  182:   },
  183: 
  184:   delete: async (courseId: number, id: number): Promise<void> => {
  185:     await api.delete(API_ENDPOINT.RESOURCE_DETAIL(courseId, id));
  186:   },
  187: 
  188:   reorder: async (
  189:     courseId: number,
  190:     items: { id: number; orderIndex: number }[],
  191:   ): Promise<void> => {
  192:     await api.put(API_ENDPOINT.RESOURCE_REORDER(courseId), { items });
  193:   },
  194: };

   82:   ): Promise<Student> => {
   83:     const { data } = await api.patch<RequestResponse<Student>>(
   84:       API_ENDPOINT.STUDENT_STATUS(id),
   85:       payload
   86:     );
   87:     return data.data;
   88:   },
   89: 
   90:   lockAccount: async (
   91:     id: number,
   92:     payload: StudentAccountLockPayload
   93:   ): Promise<Student> => {
   94:     const { data } = await api.patch<RequestResponse<Student>>(
   95:       API_ENDPOINT.STUDENT_LOCK_ACCOUNT(id),
   96:       payload
   97:     );
   98:     return data.data;
   99:   },
  100: 
  101:   addGuardian: async (
  102:     id: number,
  103:     payload: GuardianPayload
  104:   ): Promise<Guardian> => {
  105:     const { data } = await api.post<RequestResponse<Guardian>>(
  106:       API_ENDPOINT.STUDENT_GUARDIANS(id),
  107:       payload
  108:     );
  109:     return data.data;
  110:   },
  111: 
  112:   getGuardians: async (id: number): Promise<Guardian[]> => {
  113:     const { data } = await api.get<RequestResponse<Guardian[]>>(
  114:       API_ENDPOINT.STUDENT_GUARDIANS(id)
  115:     );
  116:     return data.data;
  117:   },
  118: 
  119:   deleteGuardian: async (
  120:     id: number,
  121:     guardianId: number
  122:   ): Promise<void> => {
  123:     await api.delete<RequestResponse<void>>(
  124:       API_ENDPOINT.STUDENT_GUARDIAN_DETAIL(id, guardianId)
  125:     );
  126:   },
  127: 
  128:   getAcademicHistories: async (id: number): Promise<AcademicHistory[]> => {
  129:     const { data } = await api.get<RequestResponse<AcademicHistory[]>>(
  130:       API_ENDPOINT.STUDENT_ACADEMIC_HISTORIES(id)
  131:     );
  132:     return data.data;
  133:   },
  134: 
  135:   delete: async (id: number): Promise<void> => {
  136:     await api.delete<RequestResponse<void>>(API_ENDPOINT.STUDENT_DETAIL(id));
  137:   },
  138: };
  139:

    6: export const userApi = {
    7:   getMe: async (): Promise<UserInfo> => {
    8:     const { data } = await api.get<RequestResponse<UserInfo>>(API_ENDPOINT.AUTH_ME);
    9:     return data.data;
   10:   },
   11:

   15:   },
   16: };

    1: /** Response chuẩn của BE — RequestResponse */
    2: export interface RequestResponse<T = unknown> {
    3:   status: 'success' | 'error';
    4:   timestamp: string;
    5:   message: string | null;
    6:   data: T;
    7: }
    8: 
    9: /** Response lỗi của BE — ExceptionResponse */
   10: export interface ExceptionResponse {
   11:   status: 'error';
   12:   timestamp: string;
   13:   message: string | Record<string, unknown>;
   14: }
   15: 
   16: /** Page response (Spring Data) */
   17: export interface PageResponse<T> {
   18:   content: T[];
   19:   pageable: {
   20:     pageNumber: number;
   21:     pageSize: number;
   22:     sort: { empty: boolean; sorted: boolean; unsorted: boolean };
   23:     offset: number;
   24:     paged: boolean;
   25:     unpaged: boolean;
   26:   };
   27:   last: boolean;
   28:   totalPages: number;
   29:   totalElements: number;
   30:   size: number;
   31:   number: number;
   32:   sort: { empty: boolean; sorted: boolean; unsorted: boolean };
   33:   first: boolean;
   34:   numberOfElements: number;
   35:   empty: boolean;
   36: }
   37: 
   38: /** Query params cho phân trang */
   39: export interface PageParams {
   40:   page?: number;
   41:   size?: number;
   42:   sortBy?: string;
   43:   sortDir?: 'asc' | 'desc';
   44: }

// ============================================================
// FILE: ./src/services/wsClient.ts
// ============================================================
    1: // src/services/wsClient.ts
    2: import { Client } from '@stomp/stompjs';
    3: import type { StompSubscription } from '@stomp/stompjs';
    4: import SockJS from 'sockjs-client';
    5: 
    6: type MsgCallback = (data: any) => void;
    7: type StatusListener = (connected: boolean) => void;
    8: 
    9: let client: Client | null = null;
   10: let activating = false;
   11: let connected = false;
   12: 
   13: const statusListeners = new Set<StatusListener>();
   14: const pending: Array<{
   15:   destination: string;
   16:   callback: MsgCallback;
   17:   unsub: { fn: (() => void) | null };
   18: }> = [];
   19: 
   20: const notifyStatus = (next: boolean) => {
   21:   connected = next;
   22:   statusListeners.forEach((fn) => {
   23:     try { fn(next); } catch (e) { console.warn('[WS] status listener error', e); }
   24:   });
   25: };
   26: 
   27: export const connectWs = () => {
   28:   if (client || activating) return;
   29:   activating = true;
   30: 
   31:   client = new Client({
   32:     webSocketFactory: () => new SockJS('/ws'),
   33:     reconnectDelay: 5000,
   34:     heartbeatIncoming: 10000,
   35:     heartbeatOutgoing: 10000,
   36: 
   37:     onConnect: () => {
   38:       console.log('[WS] Connected');
   39:       notifyStatus(true);
   40: 
   41:       // Flush queue
   42:       pending.forEach((p) => {
   43:         try {
   44:           const sub: StompSubscription = client!.subscribe(p.destination, (msg) => {
   45:             try { p.callback(JSON.parse(msg.body)); } catch { p.callback(msg.body); }
   46:           });
   47:           p.unsub.fn = () => sub.unsubscribe();
   48:         } catch (e) {
   49:           console.warn('[WS] flush sub error', e);
   50:         }
   51:       });
   52:       pending.length = 0;
   53:     },
   54: 
   55:     onDisconnect: () => {
   56:       console.log('[WS] Disconnected');
   57:       notifyStatus(false);
   58:     },
   59: 
   60:     onWebSocketClose: () => {
   61:       notifyStatus(false);
   62:     },
   63: 
   64:     onStompError: (frame) => {
   65:       console.warn('[WS] STOMP error:', frame.headers['message']);
   66:       notifyStatus(false);
   67:     },
   68:   });
   69: 
   70:   client.activate();
   71:   activating = false;
   72: };
   73: 
   74: export const disconnectWs = () => {
   75:   if (client) {
   76:     try { client.deactivate(); } catch { /* ignore */ }
   77:     client = null;
   78:   }
   79:   pending.length = 0;
   80:   notifyStatus(false);
   81: };
   82: 
   83: export const subscribeWs = (destination: string, callback: MsgCallback): (() => void) => {
   84:   if (client?.connected) {
   85:     const sub = client.subscribe(destination, (msg) => {
   86:       try { callback(JSON.parse(msg.body)); } catch { callback(msg.body); }
   87:     });
   88:     return () => { try { sub.unsubscribe(); } catch { /* ignore */ } };
   89:   }
   90: 
   91:   const entry = { destination, callback, unsub: { fn: null as (() => void) | null } };
   92:   pending.push(entry);
   93: 
   94:   return () => {
   95:     const idx = pending.indexOf(entry);
   96:     if (idx >= 0) pending.splice(idx, 1);
   97:     if (entry.unsub.fn) entry.unsub.fn();
   98:   };
   99: };
  100: 
  101: export const onWsStatusChange = (listener: StatusListener): (() => void) => {
  102:   statusListeners.add(listener);
  103:   listener(connected);
  104:   return () => { statusListeners.delete(listener); };
  105: };
  106: 
  107: export const getWsStatus = () => connected;

// ============================================================
// FILE: ./src/stores/authStore.ts
// ============================================================
    1: import { create } from 'zustand';
    2: import { authApi } from '@/services/api/auth';
    3: import type { LoginRequest, UserInfo } from '@/services/types/user';
    4: import { ROLE } from '@/constants/roles';
    5: 
    6: interface AuthState {
    7:   user: UserInfo | null;
    8:   isAuthenticated: boolean;
    9:   isLoading: boolean;
   10:   initialized: boolean;
   11: 
   12:   login: (payload: LoginRequest) => Promise<UserInfo>;
   13:   logout: () => Promise<void>;
   14:   fetchMe: () => Promise<UserInfo | null>;
   15:   setUser: (user: UserInfo | null) => void;
   16:   clear: () => void;
   17: 
   18:   hasRole: (role: string) => boolean;
   19:   isAdmin: () => boolean;
   20:   isInstructor: () => boolean;
   21:   isStudent: () => boolean;
   22: }
   23: 
   24: export const useAuthStore = create<AuthState>((set, get) => ({
   25:   user: null,
   26:   isAuthenticated: false,
   27:   isLoading: false,
   28:   initialized: false,
   29: 
   30:   login: async (payload) => {
   31:     set({ isLoading: true });
   32:     try {
   33:       await authApi.login(payload);
   34:       const user = await authApi.me();
   35:       set({ user, isAuthenticated: true, initialized: true });
   36:       return user;
   37:     } finally {
   38:       set({ isLoading: false });
   39:     }
   40:   },
   41: 
   42:   logout: async () => {
   43:     try {
   44:       await authApi.logout();
   45:     } catch {
   46:       // ignore
   47:     } finally {
   48:       set({ user: null, isAuthenticated: false });
   49:     }
   50:   },
   51: 
   52:   fetchMe: async () => {
   53:     try {
   54:       const user = await authApi.me();
   55:       set({ user, isAuthenticated: true, initialized: true });
   56:       return user;
   57:     } catch {
   58:       set({ user: null, isAuthenticated: false, initialized: true });
   59:       return null;
   60:     }
   61:   },
   62: 
   63:   setUser: (user) => set({ user, isAuthenticated: !!user }),
   64: 
   65:   clear: () => set({ user: null, isAuthenticated: false }),
   66: 
   67:   hasRole: (role) => get().user?.roleCode === role,
   68:   isAdmin: () => get().user?.roleCode === ROLE.ADMIN,
   69:   isInstructor: () => get().user?.roleCode === ROLE.INSTRUCTOR,
   70:   isStudent: () => get().user?.roleCode === ROLE.STUDENT,
   71: }));

// ============================================================
// FILE: ./src/stores/localeStore.ts
// ============================================================
    1: import { create } from 'zustand';
    2: import dayjs from 'dayjs';
    3: import 'dayjs/locale/vi';
    4: import 'dayjs/locale/en';
    5: 
    6: export type LocaleType = 'en' | 'vi';
    7: 
    8: interface LocaleState {
    9:   locale: LocaleType;
   10:   setLocale: (locale: LocaleType) => void;
   11: }
   12: 
   13: const getInitialLocale = (): LocaleType => {
   14:   const saved = localStorage.getItem('app_locale');
   15:   if (saved === 'en' || saved === 'vi') return saved;
   16:   return 'vi';
   17: };
   18: 
   19: const initial = getInitialLocale();
   20: dayjs.locale(initial === 'vi' ? 'vi' : 'en');
   21: 
   22: export const useLocaleStore = create<LocaleState>((set) => ({
   23:   locale: initial,
   24:   setLocale: (locale: LocaleType) => {
   25:     localStorage.setItem('app_locale', locale);
   26:     dayjs.locale(locale === 'vi' ? 'vi' : 'en');
   27:     set({ locale });
   28:   },
   29: }));
   30:

// ============================================================
// FILE: ./src/utils/format.ts
// ============================================================
    1: import dayjs from 'dayjs';
    2: import relativeTime from 'dayjs/plugin/relativeTime';
    3: import 'dayjs/locale/vi';
    4: 
    5: dayjs.extend(relativeTime);
    6: dayjs.locale('vi');
    7: 
    8: export const formatMoney = (value?: number | string | null, showFree = true): string => {
    9:   if (value === null || value === undefined || value === '') return showFree ? 'Miễn phí' : '0 đ';
   10:   const num = typeof value === 'string' ? Number(value) : value;
   11:   if (isNaN(num)) return '0 đ';
   12:   if (num === 0 && showFree) return 'Miễn phí';
   13:   return new Intl.NumberFormat('vi-VN', { style: 'currency', currency: 'VND' }).format(num);
   14: };
   15: 
   16: export const formatDate = (value?: string | Date | null, format = 'DD/MM/YYYY'): string => {
   17:   if (!value) return '—';
   18:   return dayjs(value).format(format);
   19: };
   20: 
   21: export const formatDateTime = (value?: string | Date | null): string => {
   22:   if (!value) return '—';
   23:   return dayjs(value).format('DD/MM/YYYY HH:mm');
   24: };
   25: 
   26: export const formatRelative = (value?: string | Date | null): string => {
   27:   if (!value) return '—';
   28:   return dayjs(value).fromNow();
   29: };
   30: 
   31: export const formatNumber = (value?: number | null): string => {
   32:   if (value === null || value === undefined) return '0';
   33:   return new Intl.NumberFormat('vi-VN').format(value);
   34: };
   35: 
   36: export const truncate = (text: string, max = 100): string =>
   37:   text.length > max ? `${text.slice(0, max)}...` : text;
   38: 
   39: export const getInitials = (name?: string): string => {
   40:   if (!name) return '?';
   41:   const parts = name.trim().split(/\s+/);
   42:   if (parts.length === 1) return parts[0].charAt(0).toUpperCase();
   43:   return (parts[0].charAt(0) + parts[parts.length - 1].charAt(0)).toUpperCase();
   44: };

// ============================================================
// FILE: ./src/utils/permission.ts
// ============================================================
    1: import { ROLE } from '@/constants/roles';
    2: import type { UserInfo } from '@/services/types/user';
    3: 
    4: export const hasRole = (user: UserInfo | null, role: string): boolean =>
    5:   user?.roleCode === role;
    6: 
    7: export const isAdmin = (user: UserInfo | null): boolean => hasRole(user, ROLE.ADMIN);
    8: export const isInstructor = (user: UserInfo | null): boolean => hasRole(user, ROLE.INSTRUCTOR);
    9: export const isStudent = (user: UserInfo | null): boolean => hasRole(user, ROLE.STUDENT);
   10: 
   11: export const hasAnyRole = (user: UserInfo | null, roles: string[]): boolean => {
   12:   if (!user?.roleCode) return false;
   13:   return roles.includes(user.roleCode);
   14: };

// ============================================================
// FILE: ./src/utils/storage.ts
// ============================================================
    1: const PREFIX = '@ecms';
    2: 
    3: export const storage = {
    4:   get<T = unknown>(key: string): T | null {
    5:     try {
    6:       const raw = localStorage.getItem(`${PREFIX}/${key}`);
    7:       return raw ? (JSON.parse(raw) as T) : null;
    8:     } catch {
    9:       return null;
   10:     }
   11:   },
   12: 
   13:   set(key: string, value: unknown): void {
   14:     try {
   15:       localStorage.setItem(`${PREFIX}/${key}`, JSON.stringify(value));
   16:     } catch (e) {
   17:       console.warn('[storage] set failed:', e);
   18:     }
   19:   },
   20: 
   21:   remove(key: string): void {
   22:     localStorage.removeItem(`${PREFIX}/${key}`);
   23:   },
   24: 
   25:   clear(): void {
   26:     Object.keys(localStorage)
   27:       .filter((k) => k.startsWith(`${PREFIX}/`))
   28:       .forEach((k) => localStorage.removeItem(k));
   29:   },
   30: };

// ============================================================
// FILE: ./src/utils/validators.ts
// ============================================================
    1: import { z } from 'zod';
    2: 
    3: export const emailSchema = z.email('Email không hợp lệ');
    4: 
    5: export const passwordSchema = z
    6:   .string()
    7:   .min(6, 'Mật khẩu ít nhất 6 ký tự')
    8:   .max(100, 'Mật khẩu tối đa 100 ký tự');
    9: 
   10: export const phoneSchema = z
   11:   .string()
   12:   .regex(/^\d{10,11}$/, 'Số điện thoại không hợp lệ')
   13:   .optional()
   14:   .or(z.literal(''));
   15: 
   16: export const loginSchema = z.object({
   17:   email: emailSchema,
   18:   password: z.string().min(1, 'Vui lòng nhập mật khẩu'),
   19: });
   20: 
   21: export const registerSchema = z
   22:   .object({
   23:     email: emailSchema,
   24:     password: passwordSchema,
   25:     confirmPassword: z.string().min(1, 'Vui lòng xác nhận mật khẩu'),
   26:     fullName: z.string().min(1, 'Vui lòng nhập họ tên').max(150),
   27:     phone: phoneSchema,
   28:     agreedToTerms: z.literal(true, {
   29:       message: 'Vui lòng đồng ý điều khoản',
   30:     }),
   31:   })
   32:   .refine((data) => data.password === data.confirmPassword, {
   33:     message: 'Mật khẩu xác nhận không khớp',
   34:     path: ['confirmPassword'],
   35:   });
   36: 
   37: export const changePasswordSchema = z
   38:   .object({
   39:     oldPassword: z.string().min(1, 'Vui lòng nhập mật khẩu cũ'),
   40:     newPassword: passwordSchema,
   41:     confirmPassword: z.string().min(1),
   42:   })
   43:   .refine((d) => d.newPassword === d.confirmPassword, {
   44:     message: 'Mật khẩu xác nhận không khớp',
   45:     path: ['confirmPassword'],
   46:   });
   47: 
   48: export const forgotPasswordSchema = z.object({
   49:   email: emailSchema,
   50: });
   51: 
   52: export const courseCreateSchema = z.object({
   53:   title: z.string().min(1, 'Vui lòng nhập tiêu đề').max(255),
   54:   shortDescription: z.string().max(500).optional(),
   55:   description: z.string().optional(),
   56:   thumbnailUrl: z.string().optional(),
   57:   price: z.number().min(0, 'Giá phải >= 0'),
   58:   isFree: z.boolean().optional(),
   59:   courseType: z.enum(['ONLINE', 'OFFLINE']),
   60:   categoryId: z.number().optional(),
   61: });
   62: 
   63: export type LoginForm = z.infer<typeof loginSchema>;
   64: export type RegisterForm = z.infer<typeof registerSchema>;
   65: export type ChangePasswordForm = z.infer<typeof changePasswordSchema>;
   66: export type ForgotPasswordForm = z.infer<typeof forgotPasswordSchema>;
   67: export type CourseCreateForm = z.infer<typeof courseCreateSchema>;
